0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|w|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.42816pt\hskip 17.42816pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.42816pt\hskip 17.42816ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|w|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.42816pt\hskip 17.42816pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.42816pt\hskip 17.42816ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|w|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-10.7234pt\hskip 10.7234pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-10.7234pt\hskip 10.7234ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|w|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.76285pt\hskip 9.76285pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.76285pt\hskip 9.76285ptH}}}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|v|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.20592pt\hskip 15.20592pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.20592pt\hskip 15.20592pt{V}_{s}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|v|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.20592pt\hskip 15.20592pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.20592pt\hskip 15.20592pt{V}_{s}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|v|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.00119pt\hskip 9.00119pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.00119pt\hskip 9.00119pt{V}_{s}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|v|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.3739pt\hskip 8.3739pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.3739pt\hskip 8.3739pt{V}_{s}}}},

(2.41)

and, for all
u,v\in V
,
w\in V_{s}
,

|b(u,w,v)|\leq{c}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.47806pt\hskip 9.47806pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.47806pt\hskip 9.47806ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.81377pt\hskip 8.81377pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.81377pt\hskip 8.81377ptH}}}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|w|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.42816pt\hskip 17.42816pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.42816pt\hskip 17.42816pt{V}_{s}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|w|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.42816pt\hskip 17.42816pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.42816pt\hskip 17.42816pt{V}_{s}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|w|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-10.7234pt\hskip 10.7234pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-10.7234pt\hskip 10.7234pt{V}_{s}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|w|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.76285pt\hskip 9.76285pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.76285pt\hskip 9.76285pt{V}_{s}}}}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|v|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.20592pt\hskip 15.20592pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.20592pt\hskip 15.20592ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|v|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.20592pt\hskip 15.20592pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.20592pt\hskip 15.20592ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|v|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.00119pt\hskip 9.00119pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.00119pt\hskip 9.00119ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|v|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.3739pt\hskip 8.3739pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.3739pt\hskip 8.3739ptH}}}.

(2.42)

Hence, the trilinear form
b
can be uniquely extended to a tri-linear forms, denoted by the same letter,

\displaystyle b\colon H\times H\times{V}_{s}\to\mathbb{R}

\displaystyle\mbox{ and }b\colon H\times{V}_{s}\times H\to\mathbb{R}

satisfying (
2.41
) and (
2.42
) and the operator
B
can be uniquely extended
to bounded bilinear operators

\displaystyle B\colon H\times H\to{V}_{s}^{\prime},

\displaystyle\mbox{ and }B\colon H\times{V}_{s}\to H,

(2.43)

i.e. satisfying the following estimate

\displaystyle|B(u,w){|}_{{V}_{s}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq c{|u|}_{H}{|w|}_{H},\qquad u,w\in H,

(2.44)

\displaystyle|B(u,w){|}_{H}

\displaystyle\leq c{|u|}_{H}{|w|}_{{V}_{s}},\qquad u\in H,w\in{V}_{s}.

(2.45)

We conclude this subsection in recalling properties of the form
b
with respect to the spaces

H_{R}
introduced at the end of the previous subsection.

Analogously to (
2.41
) we can show that there exists
c>0
such that

|b(u,w,v)|\leq{c}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449ptH_{R}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449ptH_{R}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.47806pt\hskip 9.47806pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.47806pt\hskip 9.47806ptH_{R}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.81377pt\hskip 8.81377pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.81377pt\hskip 8.81377ptH_{R}}}}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|w|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.42816pt\hskip 17.42816pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.42816pt\hskip 17.42816ptH_{R}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|w|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.42816pt\hskip 17.42816pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.42816pt\hskip 17.42816ptH_{R}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|w|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-10.7234pt\hskip 10.7234pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-10.7234pt\hskip 10.7234ptH_{R}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|w|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.76285pt\hskip 9.76285pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.76285pt\hskip 9.76285ptH_{R}}}}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|v|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.20592pt\hskip 15.20592pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.20592pt\hskip 15.20592pt{V}_{s}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|v|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.20592pt\hskip 15.20592pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.20592pt\hskip 15.20592pt{V}_{s}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|v|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.00119pt\hskip 9.00119pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.00119pt\hskip 9.00119pt{V}_{s}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|v|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.3739pt\hskip 8.3739pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.3739pt\hskip 8.3739pt{V}_{s}}}},\mbox{ for }u,w\in{H}\mbox{ and }v\in{V}_{s}:\supp(v)\subset B_{R}.

(2.46)

Note that the above inequality follows from inequality (
2.41
). It is essential in the identification of the limit.

2.5
Approximation of maps

Let us recall, see Section
2.2
, that
L
is a positive self-adjoint unbounded operator in
H
such that
{L}^{-1}
is compact and

D(L^{1/2})=U
isometrically, so that in particular

\displaystyle U\subset V\mbox{ continuously and densely, }

(2.47)

and the identity (
2.28
) holds.
Therefore, there exists
an orthonormal basis
\{{e}_{i}{\}}_{i=1}^{\infty}
of
{H}
consisting of eigenvectors of the operator
L
. The corresponding eigenvalues will be denoted by
\bigl(\lambda_{i}\bigr)_{i=1}^{\infty}
.
Notice that
{e}_{i}\in U
,
i\in\mathbb{N}
, because
D(L)\subset D(L^{\frac{1}{2}})=U
.
For any
n\in\mathbb{N}
let

U_{n}\coloneqq\Span\{{e}_{1},...,{e}_{n}\}

be the subspace of
U
spanned by the vectors
{e}_{1},...,{e}_{n}
.
We endow the space
U_{n}
with the norm from
U
. Let
{H}_{n}
be the space
U_{n}
endowed with the norm inherited from
{H}
. Obviously,
{H}_{n}
is a subspace of
U
, and hence of
V
.

Let
{P}_{n}
be an operator defined by

{P}_{n}:U^{\prime}\ni{u}^{\ast}\mapsto\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle{u}^{\ast},{e}_{i}\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-28.86295pt\hskip 28.86295pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-28.86295ptU^{\prime}\hskip 28.86295ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle{u}^{\ast},{e}_{i}\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-28.86295pt\hskip 28.86295pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-28.86295ptU^{\prime}\hskip 28.86295ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle{u}^{\ast},{e}_{i}\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-25.91231pt\hskip 25.91231pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-25.91231ptU^{\prime}\hskip 25.91231ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle{u}^{\ast},{e}_{i}\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-24.75563pt\hskip 24.75563pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-24.75563ptU^{\prime}\hskip 24.75563ptU}}}{e}_{i}\in U.

(2.48)

Let us observe that the range of
{P}_{n}
is equal to
U_{n}
and,
in view of (
2.31
), that

{P}_{n}u=\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bigl(u,{e}_{i}\bigr)}_{H}{e}_{i},\qquad u\in H.

(2.49)

Therefore the restriction of the operator
{P}_{n}
to the space
{H}
,
is equal to the
{H}
-orthogonal projection from
{H}

onto
{H}_{n}
. For the sake of simplicity, this restriction of
{P}_{n}
will also be denoted by
{P}_{n}
. In particular, all the operators
(P_{n})_{n=1}^{\infty}
have unit norm, i.e.

\|{P}_{n}\|_{\mathscr{L}(H)}=1,\qquad n\in\mathbb{N}.

(2.50)

The restriction of the operator
{P}_{n}
to the space
{U}
will be denoted by

\tilde{P}_{n}\coloneqq P_{n}\big|_{U}\colon\,U\to U.

Let us present a list of additional properties of the operators
P_{n}
.

Lemma 2.6
.

Assume that

n\in\mathbb{N}
. Then

The map
\tilde{P}_{n}
is an
{U}
-orthogonal projection in
{U}
(with range equal to
{U}_{n}
); in particular
\|\tilde{P}_{n}\|_{\mathscr{L}(U)}=1
.

For all
u,v\in U
and
{u}^{\ast}\in U^{\prime}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}u^{\ast},v\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.0024pt\hskip 41.0024pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-41.0024ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.0024ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}u^{\ast},v\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.0024pt\hskip 41.0024pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-41.0024ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.0024ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}u^{\ast},v\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.27324pt\hskip 34.27324pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.27324ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.27324ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}u^{\ast},v\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-32.14206pt\hskip 32.14206pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-32.14206ptU^{\prime}\hskip 32.14206ptU}}}

\displaystyle={\bigl({P}_{n}{u}^{\ast},v\bigr)}_{H}=\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle u^{\ast},\tilde{P}_{n}v\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-37.0823pt\hskip 37.0823pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-37.0823ptU^{\prime}\hskip 37.0823ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle u^{\ast},\tilde{P}_{n}v\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-37.0823pt\hskip 37.0823pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-37.0823ptU^{\prime}\hskip 37.0823ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle u^{\ast},\tilde{P}_{n}v\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-33.6611pt\hskip 33.6611pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-33.6611ptU^{\prime}\hskip 33.6611ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle u^{\ast},\tilde{P}_{n}v\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-32.45279pt\hskip 32.45279pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-32.45279ptU^{\prime}\hskip 32.45279ptU}}},

\|{P}_{n}\|_{\mathscr{L}(U^{\prime})}=1
.
Moreover,

For all

u\in U
and
{u}^{\ast}\in U^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\left|{P}_{n}u^{\ast}-u^{\ast}\right|_{U^{\prime}}=0,

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\left|\tilde{P}_{n}u-u\right|_{U}=0.

(2.51)

Proof.

Part (i). Put
\tilde{e}_{i}\coloneqq\frac{{e}_{i}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{i}}}=L^{-\frac{1}{2}}{e}_{i}
,
i\in\mathbb{N}
. Observe that since
U=D(L^{\frac{1}{2}})
isometrically the sequence
(\tilde{e}_{i})

is an orthonormal basis of
{U}
, cf. (
2.29
).
Thus, by using (
2.28
) we have for u
\in U

{\bigl(u,{e}_{i}\bigr)}_{H}{e}_{i}=\Bigl(u,\lambda_{i}\frac{{e}_{i}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{i}}}{\Bigr)}_{H}\frac{{e}_{i}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{i}}}=\Bigl(u,L\frac{{e}_{i}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{i}}}{\Bigr)}_{H}\frac{{e}_{i}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{i}}}=\Bigl(u,\frac{{e}_{i}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{i}}}{\Bigr)}_{U}\frac{{e}_{i}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{i}}},

which along with (
2.49
) imply

\tilde{P}_{n}u=\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bigl(u,\tilde{e}_{i}\bigr)}_{U}\tilde{e}_{i},\qquad u\in U.

This last equation easily implies that
\tilde{P}_{n}
is an
{U}
-orthogonal projection in
{U}
and that

\sup_{n}\|\tilde{P}_{n}\|_{\mathscr{L}(U)}\leq 1
. Thus, the proof of part (i) is complete.

Part (ii). This equality follows from identity (
2.30
).

Part (iii). According to part (i),
\tilde{P}_{n}
is an orthogonal projection in
U
and hence its operator norm is equal to
1
. Then the dual map
\tilde{P}_{n}^{\prime}\colon U^{\prime}\to U^{\prime}
also has norm
1
in
\mathscr{L}(U^{\prime})
. Therefore, it suffices to prove that
the map
P_{n}
considered as a map from
U^{\prime}
to
U^{\prime}
, denoted for the time being by
Q_{n}
, is equal to
\tilde{P}_{n}^{\prime}
. That is, we need to show that

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle Q_{n}\phi,x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-37.2447pt\hskip 37.2447pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-37.2447ptU^{\prime}\hskip 37.2447ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle Q_{n}\phi,x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-37.2447pt\hskip 37.2447pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-37.2447ptU^{\prime}\hskip 37.2447ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle Q_{n}\phi,x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-30.58653pt\hskip 30.58653pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-30.58653ptU^{\prime}\hskip 30.58653ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle Q_{n}\phi,x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-28.2736pt\hskip 28.2736pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-28.2736ptU^{\prime}\hskip 28.2736ptU}}}=\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\phi,\tilde{P}_{n}x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-33.2281pt\hskip 33.2281pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-33.2281ptU^{\prime}\hskip 33.2281ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\phi,\tilde{P}_{n}x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-33.2281pt\hskip 33.2281pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-33.2281ptU^{\prime}\hskip 33.2281ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\phi,\tilde{P}_{n}x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.84433pt\hskip 29.84433pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-29.84433ptU^{\prime}\hskip 29.84433ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\phi,\tilde{P}_{n}x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-28.41801pt\hskip 28.41801pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-28.41801ptU^{\prime}\hskip 28.41801ptU}}},\;\;\mbox{ for all }\phi\in U^{\prime},\,x\in U.

For this purpose we first observe that

Q_{n}=i^{\prime}\circ\mathrm{R}\circ i\circ P_{n},

(2.52)

where
P_{n}\colon U^{\prime}\to U
is defined by (
2.48
),
i\colon U\hookrightarrow H
is the natural embedding introduced in (
2.24
),
\mathrm{R}\colon H\to H^{\prime}
is the isometric isomorphism derived from the Riesz Lemma and
i^{\prime}\colon H^{\prime}\to U^{\prime}
is the dual map of
i
.

Next, let us choose and fix
\phi\in U^{\prime}
and
x\in U
. Then we have the following chain of equalities

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\phi,\tilde{P}_{n}x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-33.2281pt\hskip 33.2281pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-33.2281ptU^{\prime}\hskip 33.2281ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\phi,\tilde{P}_{n}x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-33.2281pt\hskip 33.2281pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-33.2281ptU^{\prime}\hskip 33.2281ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\phi,\tilde{P}_{n}x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.84433pt\hskip 29.84433pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-29.84433ptU^{\prime}\hskip 29.84433ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\phi,\tilde{P}_{n}x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-28.41801pt\hskip 28.41801pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-28.41801ptU^{\prime}\hskip 28.41801ptU}}}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\phi}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.84724pt\hskip 9.84724pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-9.84724ptU^{\prime}\hskip 9.84724pt}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\phi}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.84724pt\hskip 9.84724pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-9.84724ptU^{\prime}\hskip 9.84724pt}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\phi}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.69724pt\hskip 8.69724pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-8.69724ptU^{\prime}\hskip 8.69724pt}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\phi}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.09727pt\hskip 8.09727pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-8.09727ptU^{\prime}\hskip 8.09727pt}}},\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\bigl(x,\tilde{e}_{j}\bigr)}_{U}\tilde{e}_{j}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889ptU}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889ptU}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889ptU}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889ptU}}}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\phi}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.84724pt\hskip 9.84724pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-9.84724ptU^{\prime}\hskip 9.84724pt}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\phi}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.84724pt\hskip 9.84724pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-9.84724ptU^{\prime}\hskip 9.84724pt}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\phi}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.69724pt\hskip 8.69724pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-8.69724ptU^{\prime}\hskip 8.69724pt}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\phi}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.09727pt\hskip 8.09727pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-8.09727ptU^{\prime}\hskip 8.09727pt}}},\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\bigl(x,{e}_{j}\bigr)}_{H}{e}_{j}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889ptU}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889ptU}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889ptU}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip 3.8889ptU}}}=\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\bigl(x,{e}_{j}\bigr)}_{H}\;\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\phi,{e}_{j}\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-25.38376pt\hskip 25.38376pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-25.38376ptU^{\prime}\hskip 25.38376ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\phi,{e}_{j}\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-25.38376pt\hskip 25.38376pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-25.38376ptU^{\prime}\hskip 25.38376ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\phi,{e}_{j}\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-22.38pt\hskip 22.38pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-22.38ptU^{\prime}\hskip 22.38ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\phi,{e}_{j}\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-21.12103pt\hskip 21.12103pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-21.12103ptU^{\prime}\hskip 21.12103ptU}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\phi,{e}_{j}\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-25.38376pt\hskip 25.38376pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-25.38376ptU^{\prime}\hskip 25.38376ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\phi,{e}_{j}\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-25.38376pt\hskip 25.38376pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-25.38376ptU^{\prime}\hskip 25.38376ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\phi,{e}_{j}\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-22.38pt\hskip 22.38pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-22.38ptU^{\prime}\hskip 22.38ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\phi,{e}_{j}\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-21.12103pt\hskip 21.12103pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-21.12103ptU^{\prime}\hskip 21.12103ptU}}}\;{\bigl(i{e}_{j},ix\bigr)}_{H}=\bigg(i\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\phi,{e}_{j}\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-25.38376pt\hskip 25.38376pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-25.38376ptU^{\prime}\hskip 25.38376ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\phi,{e}_{j}\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-25.38376pt\hskip 25.38376pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-25.38376ptU^{\prime}\hskip 25.38376ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\phi,{e}_{j}\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-22.38pt\hskip 22.38pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-22.38ptU^{\prime}\hskip 22.38ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\phi,{e}_{j}\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-21.12103pt\hskip 21.12103pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-21.12103ptU^{\prime}\hskip 21.12103ptU}}}{e}_{j}\Big),ix\bigg)_{H}

\displaystyle={\bigl(i(P_{n}\phi),ix\bigr)}_{H}=\mathchoice{{\hskip 9.78961pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle\mathrm{R}i(P_{n}\phi),ix\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-59.17737pt\hskip 59.17737pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.78961pt\hskip-59.17737ptH^{\prime}\hskip 59.17737ptH}}}{{\hskip 9.78961pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle\mathrm{R}i(P_{n}\phi),ix\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-59.17737pt\hskip 59.17737pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.78961pt\hskip-59.17737ptH^{\prime}\hskip 59.17737ptH}}}{{\hskip 8.62854pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle\mathrm{R}i(P_{n}\phi),ix\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-48.17064pt\hskip 48.17064pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.62854pt\hskip-48.17064ptH^{\prime}\hskip 48.17064ptH}}}{{\hskip 8.62854pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle\mathrm{R}i(P_{n}\phi),ix\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.707pt\hskip 43.707pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.62854pt\hskip-43.707ptH^{\prime}\hskip 43.707ptH}}}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle i^{\prime}\mathrm{R}i(P_{n}\phi),x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-61.98293pt\hskip 61.98293pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-61.98293ptU^{\prime}\hskip 61.98293ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle i^{\prime}\mathrm{R}i(P_{n}\phi),x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-61.98293pt\hskip 61.98293pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-61.98293ptU^{\prime}\hskip 61.98293ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle i^{\prime}\mathrm{R}i(P_{n}\phi),x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-50.87552pt\hskip 50.87552pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-50.87552ptU^{\prime}\hskip 50.87552ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle i^{\prime}\mathrm{R}i(P_{n}\phi),x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-46.41188pt\hskip 46.41188pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-46.41188ptU^{\prime}\hskip 46.41188ptU}}}=\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle Q_{n}\phi,x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-37.2447pt\hskip 37.2447pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-37.2447ptU^{\prime}\hskip 37.2447ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle Q_{n}\phi,x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-37.2447pt\hskip 37.2447pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-37.2447ptU^{\prime}\hskip 37.2447ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle Q_{n}\phi,x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-30.58653pt\hskip 30.58653pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-30.58653ptU^{\prime}\hskip 30.58653ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle Q_{n}\phi,x\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-28.2736pt\hskip 28.2736pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-28.2736ptU^{\prime}\hskip 28.2736ptU}}}.

The last line completes the proof of the part (iii).

Part (iv). The second convergence was proved in
[
17
, Lemma 2.3 and 2.4]
. In order to prove the first one
let us choose and fix
u^{\ast}\in U^{\prime}
. By the density of the embedding
U\subset U^{\prime}
there exists
(u_{k})_{k\in\mathbb{N}}\subset U
such that
u_{k}\to u^{\ast}
in
U^{\prime}
. From part (iii) we see that

\displaystyle\lvert P_{n}u^{\ast}-u^{\ast}\rvert_{U^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq\lVert P_{n}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}(U^{\prime})}\lvert u^{\ast}-u_{k}\rvert_{U^{\prime}}+\lvert P_{n}u_{k}-u_{k}\rvert_{U^{\prime}}+\lvert u_{k}-u^{\ast}\rvert_{U^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\lvert u_{k}-u^{\ast}\rvert_{U^{\prime}}+C\lvert P_{n}u_{k}-u_{k}\rvert_{U}.

From assertion (
2.51
) we infer that
\lim_{n\to\infty}\left|{P}_{n}u^{\ast}-u^{\ast}\right|_{U^{\prime}}=0
. This completes the proof of part (iv) and the lemma.
∎

Remark 2.7
.

(i)

Although by Lemma
2.6
the operators
(P_{n})_{n=1}^{\infty}
are contractions and hence uniformly bounded in
U
,
H
and
U^{\prime}
,
we do not know whether these operators are uniformly bounded in the spaces
V
or
V_{s}
. In particular, we do not know whether the following are true:

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|{P}_{n}u-u|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.25693pt\hskip 40.25693pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.25693pt\hskip 40.25693ptV}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|{P}_{n}u-u|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.25693pt\hskip 40.25693pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.25693pt\hskip 40.25693ptV}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|{P}_{n}u-u|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.97227pt\hskip 27.97227pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.97227pt\hskip 27.97227ptV}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|{P}_{n}u-u|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-25.55414pt\hskip 25.55414pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-25.55414pt\hskip 25.55414ptV}}}=0,\;\;u\in V,

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|{P}_{n}u-u|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.25693pt\hskip 40.25693pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.25693pt\hskip 40.25693ptV_{s}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|{P}_{n}u-u|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.25693pt\hskip 40.25693pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.25693pt\hskip 40.25693ptV_{s}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|{P}_{n}u-u|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.97227pt\hskip 27.97227pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.97227pt\hskip 27.97227ptV_{s}}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|{P}_{n}u-u|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-25.55414pt\hskip 25.55414pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-25.55414pt\hskip 25.55414ptV_{s}}}}=0,\;\;u\in V_{s}.

Of course, since both
V\cup V_{s}\subset U^{\prime}
, the expressions of the LHSs above make sense.

(ii)

On the other hand, it follows from part (iv) of Lemma
2.6
and (
2.25
) that
u\in U
,

\tilde{P}_{n}u\to u
in
V
.

Now, let us choose and fix a sequence
({\theta}_{n})_{n=1}^{\infty}
of
{\mathscr{C}}^{\infty}
-class functions
{\theta}_{n}\colon\mathbb{R}_{+}\to[0,1]
, such that

\mathds{1}_{[0,n]}\leq{\theta}_{n}\leq\mathds{1}_{[0,n+1]},\;\;n\in\mathbb{N}.

(2.53)

Next, for every
n\in\mathbb{N}
we define the following nonlinear functions

\displaystyle B_{n}\colon{H}_{n}\times{H}_{n}\ni(u,v)

\displaystyle\mapsto{\theta}_{n}(|u{|}_{U^{\prime}}){P}_{n}\bigl(B(u,v)\bigr)\in{H}_{n}

(2.54)

where
{P}_{n}\colon H\to H_{n}
. In view of (
2.43
) and since
H_{n}\subset V_{s}
, the map
B_{n}
is of
\mathscr{C}^{\infty}
-class. Note that contrary to the map
B
, the function
B_{n}
is no longer bilinear.

With an intentional abuse of notation we will denote by
B_{n}
the map

B_{n}\colon{H}_{n}\ni u\mapsto{B}_{n}(u,u)\in{H}_{n}.

(2.55)

Obviously, this map is also of
\mathscr{C}^{\infty}
-class and Lipschitz on balls.

We also define the following linear maps

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{n}\colon{H}_{n}\ni u

\displaystyle\mapsto({P}_{n}\circ\iota^{\prime})\mathscr{A}u\in{H}_{n},

(2.56)

where now
{P}_{n}\colon U^{\prime}\to H_{n}
,

\iota^{\prime}:V^{\prime}\hookrightarrow U^{\prime}
is the dual of the map

\iota:U\hookrightarrow V
introduced in (
2.24
).
Hence,
\mathscr{A}_{n}
is well defined and the map
\mathscr{A}_{n}\colon{H}_{n}\to{H}_{n}
is linear and bounded.

Let us also note the following useful identities involving the map operators
{B}_{n}
and
\mathscr{A}_{n}
that will be used later in the paper. If
u,v,w\in H_{n}
, then

\displaystyle{\bigl(B_{n}(u,v),w\bigr)}_{H_{n}}

\displaystyle={\bigl(B_{n}(u,v),w\bigr)}_{H}=\mathchoice{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle B_{n}(u,v),w\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-56.3334pt\hskip 56.3334pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-56.3334ptV^{\prime}\hskip 56.3334ptV}}}{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle B_{n}(u,v),w\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-56.3334pt\hskip 56.3334pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-56.3334ptV^{\prime}\hskip 56.3334ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle B_{n}(u,v),w\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-44.79527pt\hskip 44.79527pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-44.79527ptV^{\prime}\hskip 44.79527ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle B_{n}(u,v),w\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.7452pt\hskip 40.7452pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-40.7452ptV^{\prime}\hskip 40.7452ptV}}}{}=\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle B_{n}(u,v),w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-56.3334pt\hskip 56.3334pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-56.3334ptU^{\prime}\hskip 56.3334ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle B_{n}(u,v),w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-56.3334pt\hskip 56.3334pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-56.3334ptU^{\prime}\hskip 56.3334ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle B_{n}(u,v),w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-44.79527pt\hskip 44.79527pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-44.79527ptU^{\prime}\hskip 44.79527ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle B_{n}(u,v),w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.7452pt\hskip 40.7452pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-40.7452ptU^{\prime}\hskip 40.7452ptU}}}

\displaystyle={\theta}_{n}(|u{|}_{U^{\prime}})\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}B(u,v),w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-64.14244pt\hskip 64.14244pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-64.14244ptU^{\prime}\hskip 64.14244ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}B(u,v),w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-64.14244pt\hskip 64.14244pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-64.14244ptU^{\prime}\hskip 64.14244ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}B(u,v),w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-50.96298pt\hskip 50.96298pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-50.96298ptU^{\prime}\hskip 50.96298ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}B(u,v),w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-45.99004pt\hskip 45.99004pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-45.99004ptU^{\prime}\hskip 45.99004ptU}}}={\theta}_{n}(|u{|}_{U^{\prime}})\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle B(u,v),\tilde{P}_{n}w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-60.22235pt\hskip 60.22235pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-60.22235ptU^{\prime}\hskip 60.22235ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle B(u,v),\tilde{P}_{n}w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-60.22235pt\hskip 60.22235pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-60.22235ptU^{\prime}\hskip 60.22235ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle B(u,v),\tilde{P}_{n}w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-50.35085pt\hskip 50.35085pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-50.35085ptU^{\prime}\hskip 50.35085ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle B(u,v),\tilde{P}_{n}w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-46.30077pt\hskip 46.30077pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-46.30077ptU^{\prime}\hskip 46.30077ptU}}}

\displaystyle={\theta}_{n}(|u{|}_{U^{\prime}})\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle B(u,v),w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-50.89008pt\hskip 50.89008pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-50.89008ptU^{\prime}\hskip 50.89008ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle B(u,v),w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-50.89008pt\hskip 50.89008pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-50.89008ptU^{\prime}\hskip 50.89008ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle B(u,v),w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-39.89128pt\hskip 39.89128pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-39.89128ptU^{\prime}\hskip 39.89128ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle B(u,v),w\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.8412pt\hskip 35.8412pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-35.8412ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.8412ptU}}}={\theta}_{n}(|u{|}_{U^{\prime}})b(u,v,w).

Similarly, we have the following useful identities involving the linear maps
\mathscr{A}_{n}
. If

, then

(2.58)

3
Statement of the problem

With the notation introduced in the previous section we can rewrite Problem (
1.1
) into the following abstract stochastic evolution equations

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}u(t)

\displaystyle+\bigl(\mathscr{A}u(t)+B\bigl(u(t)\bigr)\bigr)\,\mathrm{d}t

(3.1)

\displaystyle=f(t)\,\mathrm{d}t+\int_{Y}F(t,u(t),y)\,\tilde{\eta}(\mathrm{d}t,\mathrm{d}y),\;\;t\in[0,T]

\displaystyle u(0)

\displaystyle={u}_{0}.

Before we give a precise definition of a solution to equation (
3.1
) let us emphasize
that Equation (
3.1
) is derived from (
1.1
) by applying the Leray-Helmholtz projection
P
to the latter. Since
the gradient vector fields belong to the kernel of
P
, the term
\nabla p
disappears. Let us present an alternative, but based on the same principles,
explanation how one can deduce equation (
3.1
) from (
1.1
). Multiplying the latter equation by an arbitrary test function
\phi\in\mathscr{V}
and employing the identities (
2.38
), (
2.39
) and (
2.15
), we arrive at the following Itô integral equation

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle u(t),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.29405pt\hskip 35.29405pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-35.29405ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.29405ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle u(t),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.29405pt\hskip 35.29405pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-35.29405ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.29405ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle u(t),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-28.84897pt\hskip 28.84897pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-28.84897ptU^{\prime}\hskip 28.84897ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle u(t),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-26.46552pt\hskip 26.46552pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-26.46552ptU^{\prime}\hskip 26.46552ptU}}}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}u(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.87045pt\hskip 43.87045pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-43.87045ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.87045ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}u(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.87045pt\hskip 43.87045pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-43.87045ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.87045ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}u(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.50526pt\hskip 35.50526pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-35.50526ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.50526ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}u(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-31.90079pt\hskip 31.90079pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-31.90079ptU^{\prime}\hskip 31.90079ptU}}}\,\mathrm{d}s+b(u(s),u(s),\phi)\,\mathrm{d}s\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle u_{0},\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-28.39127pt\hskip 28.39127pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-28.39127ptU^{\prime}\hskip 28.39127ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle u_{0},\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-28.39127pt\hskip 28.39127pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-28.39127ptU^{\prime}\hskip 28.39127ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle u_{0},\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-23.48093pt\hskip 23.48093pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-23.48093ptU^{\prime}\hskip 23.48093ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle u_{0},\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-22.13332pt\hskip 22.13332pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-22.13332ptU^{\prime}\hskip 22.13332ptU}}}

(3.2)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle f(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.61812pt\hskip 36.61812pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-36.61812ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.61812ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle f(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.61812pt\hskip 36.61812pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-36.61812ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.61812ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle f(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.55847pt\hskip 29.55847pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-29.55847ptU^{\prime}\hskip 29.55847ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle f(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-26.77798pt\hskip 26.77798pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-26.77798ptU^{\prime}\hskip 26.77798ptU}}}\,\mathrm{d}s+\int_{Y}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle F(s,u(s),y),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-70.80562pt\hskip 70.80562pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-70.80562ptU^{\prime}\hskip 70.80562ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle F(s,u(s),y),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-70.80562pt\hskip 70.80562pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-70.80562ptU^{\prime}\hskip 70.80562ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle F(s,u(s),y),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-54.6093pt\hskip 54.6093pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-54.6093ptU^{\prime}\hskip 54.6093ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle F(s,u(s),y),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-48.83032pt\hskip 48.83032pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-48.83032ptU^{\prime}\hskip 48.83032ptU}}}\,\tilde{\eta}(\mathrm{d}s,\mathrm{d}y),\;\;t\geq 0.

The above is the weak form of equation (
3.1
).

We are going now to present the space in which the solution will live and a list of assumptions.

3.1
Assumptions

The following spaces have been introduced in
[
17
]
,
[
47
]
. They are
similar to some spaces considered in
[
46
]
.

•

{L}_{w}^{2}(0,T;V)
is the space
{L}^{2}(0,T;V)
endowed with the weak topology which will be denoted by
\mathcal{T}_{1}
.

•

{L}^{2}(0,T;{H}_{\mathrm{loc}})
is the space of (equivalence classes of) Borel measurable functions
u\colon[0,T]\to H
such that for every
R\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle{q}_{T,R}(u)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449pt{L}^{2}(0,T;H_{R})}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449pt{L}^{2}(0,T;H_{R})}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.47806pt\hskip 9.47806pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.47806pt\hskip 9.47806pt{L}^{2}(0,T;H_{R})}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.81377pt\hskip 8.81377pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.81377pt\hskip 8.81377pt{L}^{2}(0,T;H_{R})}}}\coloneqq\biggl(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{B_{R}}|u(t,x){|}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}t{\biggr)}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=\biggl(\int_{0}^{T}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|u(t)|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.1134pt\hskip 27.1134pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.1134pt\hskip 27.1134pt{L}^{2}(B_{R})}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|u(t)|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.1134pt\hskip 27.1134pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.1134pt\hskip 27.1134pt{L}^{2}(B_{R})}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|u(t)|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-18.74893pt\hskip 18.74893pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-18.74893pt\hskip 18.74893pt{L}^{2}(B_{R})}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|u(t)|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.0488pt\hskip 17.0488pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.0488pt\hskip 17.0488pt{L}^{2}(B_{R})}}}\,\mathrm{d}t{\biggr)}^{\frac{1}{2}}<\infty,

where the spaces
H_{R}
have been defined in (
2.35
).
The topology generated by the seminorm
({q}_{T,R}{)}_{R\in\mathbb{N}}
will be denoted by
\mathcal{T}_{2}
. Let us note
{L}^{2}(0,T;{H}_{\mathrm{loc}})
is not complete, and hence neither a Fréchet, space but
{L}^{2}(0,T;{H}_{\mathrm{loc}})\cap{L}_{w}^{2}(0,T;H)
is a Fréchet space.

•

\mathbb{D}([0,T];U^{\prime})
is the space of càdlàg functions
u\colon[0,T]\to U^{\prime}
endowed with the Skorohod topology which will be denoted by
\mathcal{T}_{3}
.

•

\mathbb{D}([0,T];{H}_{w})
is the space of càdlàg functions
u\colon[0,T]\to{H}_{w}
, where the space
{H}_{w}
is equal to the space
{H}
but endowed with its weak topology. The Skorokhod topology on
\mathbb{D}([0,T];{H}_{w})
is denoted by
\mathcal{T}_{4}
.

Let us note, as it can be easily proved, that
{L}^{2}(0,T;{H}_{\mathrm{loc}})
is a separable Fréchet space, see
[
55
, Def. 1.8]
.

We now introduce
the following topological vector space

\mathscr{Z}_{T}:={L}_{w}^{2}(0,T;V)\cap{L}^{2}(0,T;{H}_{\mathrm{loc}})\cap\mathbb{D}([0,T];U^{\prime})\cap\mathbb{D}([0,T];{H}_{w})

(3.3)

whose topology
\mathcal{T}
is the topology generated by the four topologies
\mathcal{T}_{i}
,
i=1,2,3,4
.

Assumption P. 1
.

(Y,\mathscr{Y})
is a standard measurable space, that is, it is Borel-isomorphic to
\mathbb{N}
or
\{0,1\}^{\mathbb{N}}
, see
[
34
, Definition I.3.3]
, and
\nu
is a
\sigma
-finite measure on
(Y,\mathscr{Y})
.

Remark 3.1
.

We believe that Assumption P.
1
can be relaxed by assuming that
(Y,\mathscr{Y})
is a Blackwell space, see
[
35
, Section II.1]
or
[
7
, Section 6.10(iv)]
for the definition and properties.

Assumption F. 1
.

The map
F\colon[0,T]\times U^{\prime}\times Y\to U^{\prime}

is a measurable function.

Assumption F. 2
.

The restriction

F\colon[0,T]\times H\times Y\to H
of the function
F

is a well defined and a measurable function.
There exist constants
L>0
and
C_{2}>0
such that

\displaystyle\int_{Y}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|F(t,{u}_{1},y)-F(t,{u}_{2},y)|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-101.58333pt\hskip 101.58333pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-101.58333pt\hskip 101.58333ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|F(t,{u}_{1},y)-F(t,{u}_{2},y)|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-101.58333pt\hskip 101.58333pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-101.58333pt\hskip 101.58333ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|F(t,{u}_{1},y)-F(t,{u}_{2},y)|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-73.21298pt\hskip 73.21298pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-73.21298pt\hskip 73.21298ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|F(t,{u}_{1},y)-F(t,{u}_{2},y)|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-65.81094pt\hskip 65.81094pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-65.81094pt\hskip 65.81094ptH}}}\,\nu(\mathrm{d}y)

\displaystyle\leq L\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|u_{1}-{u}_{2}|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-31.53249pt\hskip 31.53249pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-31.53249pt\hskip 31.53249ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|u_{1}-{u}_{2}|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-31.53249pt\hskip 31.53249pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-31.53249pt\hskip 31.53249ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|u_{1}-{u}_{2}|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-24.70624pt\hskip 24.70624pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-24.70624pt\hskip 24.70624ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|u_{1}-{u}_{2}|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-23.21097pt\hskip 23.21097pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-23.21097pt\hskip 23.21097ptH}}},\;{u}_{1},\!{u}_{2}\in H,\;t\in[0,T],

(3.4)

\displaystyle\int_{Y}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|F(t,u,y)|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-49.08328pt\hskip 49.08328pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-49.08328pt\hskip 49.08328ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|F(t,u,y)|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-49.08328pt\hskip 49.08328pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-49.08328pt\hskip 49.08328ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|F(t,u,y)|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-33.73143pt\hskip 33.73143pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-33.73143pt\hskip 33.73143ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|F(t,u,y)|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-30.11375pt\hskip 30.11375pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-30.11375pt\hskip 30.11375ptH}}}\,\nu(\mathrm{d}y)

\displaystyle\leq{C}_{2}(1+\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-15.72449pt\hskip 15.72449ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.47806pt\hskip 9.47806pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-9.47806pt\hskip 9.47806ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|u|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.81377pt\hskip 8.81377pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-8.81377pt\hskip 8.81377ptH}}}),\;u\in H,\;t\in[0,T],

(3.5)

Assumption F. 3
.

Moreover, for every
\phi\in\mathscr{V}
the following Nemytski map
{\tilde{F}}_{\phi}
associated to
F
and defined by

\displaystyle{\tilde{F}}_{\phi}\colon\mathscr{Z}_{T}\ni u

\displaystyle\in{L}^{2}([0,T]\times Y,\Leb\otimes\nu;\mathbb{R})

Assumption F. 4
.

The following Nemytski map
F
associated to map
F
defined by

\displaystyle\textbf{F}\colon\mathscr{Z}_{T}\ni u\mapsto\bigl\{[0,T]\times Y\ni(t,y)
