\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Y}\langle{P}_{n}F(s,{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),y),\phi\rangle^{2}\,\nu(\mathrm{d}y)\,\mathrm{d}s,\;\;t\in[0,T].

Definition 5.2
.

Let
\mathscr{N}
be the family of
\overline{\mathbb{P}}
-null sets of the
\sigma
-field
\overline{\mathscr{F}}
. In what follows we will introduce a set of auxiliary filtrations.
For every
n\in\mathbb{N}
,
let
\mathbb{H}_{n}=(\mathscr{H}_{n,s}:s\geq 0)
,

\mathbb{H}_{n}^{+}=(\mathscr{H}_{n,t}^{+}:t\geq 0)
,
{\overline{\mathbb{F}}_{n}}=({\overline{\mathscr{F}}}_{n,t}:t\geq 0)

be the filtrations defined by

\displaystyle\mathscr{H}_{n,s}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\sigma\{{\overline{u}}_{n}(r),\,\,r\leq s\},\;\;s\geq 0,

(5.9)

\displaystyle\mathscr{H}_{n,t}^{+}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigcap_{s>t}\mathscr{H}_{n,s},\;\;t\geq 0,

(5.10)

\displaystyle{\overline{\mathscr{F}}}_{n,t}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\sigma\bigl(\mathscr{H}_{n,t}^{+}\cup\mathscr{N}\bigr),\;\;t\geq 0,

(5.11)

We have the following result.

Proposition 5.3
.

The filtration
{\overline{\mathbb{F}}_{n}}
is the
(\overline{\mathscr{F}},\overline{\mathbb{P}})
-completion,
in the sense of
[
35
, 1.4]
, of the right-continuous filtration
\bigl(\mathscr{H}_{n,t}^{+}\bigr)_{t\geq 0}
and
the following equality holds

{\overline{\mathscr{F}}}_{n,t}=\bigcap_{s>t}\sigma\bigl(\mathscr{H}_{n,s}\cup\mathscr{N}\bigr).

(5.12)

In particular, the filtration
{\overline{\mathbb{F}}_{n}}
is the usual augmentation of filtration
\mathbb{H}_{n}
and satisfies the usual hypothesis, see
[
35
, 1.4]
.

Proof.

Equality (
5.12
) follows by the argument as in the proof of Lemma
E.2
, see also
[
38
, Lemma 7.8]
.
∎

We begin with the following three more auxiliary results.

Lemma 5.4
.

The function

D([0,T],U^{\prime})\ni z\mapsto{\theta}_{n}(|z(\cdot){|}_{U^{\prime}})\in D([0,T],\mathbb{R})

is continuous.

Proof.

Let
z_{k}\to z
in
D([0,T],U^{\prime})
. By the standard characterisation of the Skorokhod topology, see e.g.
[
35
, Theorem VI.1.14, p. 328]
, we know that there exists a sequence
\lambda_{k}:[0,T]\to[0.T]
such that

\lim_{k\to+\infty}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}|\lambda_{k}(t)-t|\to 0,\qquad\lim_{k\to+\infty}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}|z_{k}(\lambda_{k}(t))-z(t)|_{U^{\prime}}\to 0.

It follows from the uniform continuity of
\theta_{n}
that

\lim_{k\to+\infty}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}|\theta_{n}(|z_{k}(\lambda_{k}(t))|_{U^{\prime}})-\theta_{n}(|z(t)|_{U^{\prime}})|\to 0.

Thus
\theta_{n}(|z_{k}|_{U^{\prime}})\to\theta_{n}(|z|_{U^{\prime}})
in
D([0,T],\mathbb{R})
.
∎

Lemma 5.5
.

Assume that
u\in{L}^{2}(0,T;H)
and
{({u}_{n})}_{n}
is a bounded sequence in
{L}^{2}(0,T;H)
such that
{u}_{n}\to u
in
{L}^{2}(0,T;{H}_{\mathrm{loc}})
. Let

s
be chosen as in (
2.18
).
Then for every
\psi\in{V}_{s}
,

\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{0}^{T}|\mathchoice{{\hskip 10.2952pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle B\bigl({u}_{n}(r),{u}_{n}(r)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-86.20967pt\hskip 86.20967pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-10.2952pt\hskip-86.20967ptV_{s}^{\prime}\hskip 86.20967ptV_{s}}}}{{\hskip 10.2952pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle B\bigl({u}_{n}(r),{u}_{n}(r)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-86.20967pt\hskip 86.20967pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-10.2952pt\hskip-86.20967ptV_{s}^{\prime}\hskip 86.20967ptV_{s}}}}{{\hskip 9.37859pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle B\bigl({u}_{n}(r),{u}_{n}(r)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-72.73976pt\hskip 72.73976pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-9.37859pt\hskip-72.73976ptV_{s}^{\prime}\hskip 72.73976ptV_{s}}}}{{\hskip 9.37859pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle B\bigl({u}_{n}(r),{u}_{n}(r)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-67.1782pt\hskip 67.1782pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-9.37859pt\hskip-67.1782ptV_{s}^{\prime}\hskip 67.1782ptV_{s}}}}\,-\mathchoice{{\hskip 10.2952pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle B\bigl(u(r),u(r)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-76.98964pt\hskip 76.98964pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-10.2952pt\hskip-76.98964ptV_{s}^{\prime}\hskip 76.98964ptV_{s}}}}{{\hskip 10.2952pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle B\bigl(u(r),u(r)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-76.98964pt\hskip 76.98964pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-10.2952pt\hskip-76.98964ptV_{s}^{\prime}\hskip 76.98964ptV_{s}}}}{{\hskip 9.37859pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle B\bigl(u(r),u(r)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-62.93178pt\hskip 62.93178pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-9.37859pt\hskip-62.93178ptV_{s}^{\prime}\hskip 62.93178ptV_{s}}}}{{\hskip 9.37859pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle B\bigl(u(r),u(r)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-57.37022pt\hskip 57.37022pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-9.37859pt\hskip-57.37022ptV_{s}^{\prime}\hskip 57.37022ptV_{s}}}}\,|\,dr.

(5.13)

Proof.

See
[
17
, Lemma B.1]
. But note that our result is stronger although the proof is essentially the same.
∎

Lemma 5.6
.

Assume that
x_{n}\to x
in
D([0,T])
and
u_{n}\to u
in
L^{1}(0,T)
. Then

\int_{0}^{T}x_{n}(t)u_{n}(t)\,\mathrm{d}t\to\int_{0}^{T}x(t)u(t)\,\mathrm{d}t

Proof.

There exist positive constant
A>0
and
C_{k}>0
such that

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle\coloneqq\sup_{k}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}|x_{k}(t)|<\infty,

(5.14)

\displaystyle C_{k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\int_{0}^{T}|u_{k}(t)-u(t)|\,\mathrm{d}t\to 0.

(5.15)

Moreover, the set
C
of discontinuities of function
x
is at most countable and by
[
35
, Proposition VI.2.1 (b.5)]
,

x_{k}(t)\to x(t)\mbox{ for every }t\in[0,T]\setminus C.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{T}x_{k}(t)u_{k}(t)\,\mathrm{d}t-\int_{0}^{T}x(t)u(t)\,\mathrm{d}t\right|

(5.16)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{T}|x_{k}(t)-x(t)||u(t)|\,\mathrm{d}t+\int_{0}^{T}|x_{k}(t))||u_{k}(t)-u(t)|\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{T}|x_{k}(t)-x(t)||u(t)|\,\mathrm{d}t+AC_{k}.

(5.17)

By assumptions,
C_{k}\to 0
while the first integral on the right-hand side above converges to
0
by the Lebesgue Dominated Convergence Theorem because the integrand
|x_{k}(t)-x(t)||u(t)|
(i) converges to
0
for almost all
t\in[0,T]
and (ii) is bounded by the integrable function
t\mapsto 2B|u(t)|
for some constant
B>0
.
∎

Lemma 5.7
.

Let
\psi\in U
and
n\in\mathbb{N}
be fixed. Then, the map

J^{n}_{s,t,\psi}\colon\mathscr{Z}_{T}\ni z\mapsto\int_{s}^{t}{\theta}_{n}(|z(r){|}_{U^{\prime}})\;b(z(r),z(r),\psi)\,\mathrm{d}r\in\mathbb{R}

(5.18)

is measurable.

Let us point out that in view of inequality (
2.41
), the assertion in Lemma
5.7
makes sense.

Proof of Lemma
5.7
.

Let
\psi\in U
and
n\in\mathbb{N}
be fixed. It is sufficient to prove measurability of
J^{n}_{s,t,\psi}
restricted to every subset
\mathscr{Z}_{T}^{m}
of
\mathscr{Z}_{T}
, where

\mathscr{Z}_{T}^{m}\coloneqq\bigl\{z\in\mathscr{Z}_{T}:|z|_{L^{2}(0,T;V)}\leq m,|z|_{L^{\infty}([0,T];{H})}\leq m\bigr\}

(5.19)

endowed with the trace topology from
\mathscr{Z}_{T}
.
Note that the space
\mathscr{Z}_{T}^{m}
is metrizable and hence continuity of
J^{n}_{s,t,\psi}
restricted to

\mathscr{Z}_{T}^{m}
is equivalent to the sequential continuity.

Let us choose and fix a sequence
(x_{\ell})_{\ell\in\mathbb{N}}\subset\mathscr{Z}^{m}_{T}
such that
x_{\ell}\to x\mbox{ in }\mathscr{Z}_{T}^{m}
.
By the definition of the space
\mathscr{Z}_{T}^{m}
we see that the sequence
(x_{\ell})_{\ell\in\mathbb{N}}\subset L^{2}(0,T;H)
is bounded and
x_{\ell}\to x
in
L^{2}(0,T;H_{\mathrm{loc}}).

Hence, by Lemma
5.5
we infer that
b(x_{\ell},x_{\ell},\psi)\to b(x,x,\psi)
in
L^{1}(0,T)
.

On the other hand, by Lemma

5.4
,

{\theta}_{n}(|x_{\ell}(\cdot){|}_{U^{\prime}})\to{\theta}_{n}(|x(\cdot){|}_{U^{\prime}})\mbox{ in }D([0,T]).

Therefore, by Lemma
5.6
, we deduce that

\int_{0}^{T}{\theta}_{n}(|x_{\ell}(t){|}_{U^{\prime}})b(x_{\ell}{(t)},x_{\ell}{(t)},\psi)\,\mathrm{d}t\to\int_{0}^{T}{\theta}_{n}(|x(t){|}_{U^{\prime}})b(x{(t)},x{(t)},\psi)\,\mathrm{d}t.

Consequently, the map
J^{n}_{s,t,\psi}
is lower semi-continuous, and hence measurable.
∎

The following results are consequences, via Lemma
5.1
, of our Assumptions F.
2

and F.
5
.

Lemma 5.8
.

(i)

For every
n\in\mathbb{N}
, the process
{\overline{M}}_{n}
is a square integrable
U^{\prime}
-valued
{\overline{\mathbb{F}}}_{n}
-martingale. Moreover,

\sup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\lvert\overline{M}_{n}(t)\rvert^{2}_{U^{\prime}}\right]<\infty.

(5.20)

(ii)

Moreover, if Assumption F.
5
is satisfied, then for every
p\in[2,4+\gamma]
and for every
n\in\mathbb{N}
, the process

{\overline{M}}_{n}
is a
p
-integrable
U^{\prime}
-valued
{\overline{\mathbb{F}}}_{n}
-martingale. Moreover,

\sup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\lvert\overline{M}_{n}(t)\rvert^{p}_{U^{\prime}}\right]<\infty.

(5.21)

Proof of Lemma
5.8
.

We assume here that
p
is a fixed exponent belonging to the interval
[2,4+\gamma]
.
For
p=2
we will use Lemma
5.1
while for
p\in(2,4+\gamma]
we will use the second part of Lemma
5.1
.

Following
[
35
]
we denote by
\mathscr{D}_{t}^{0}
the
\sigma
-algebra generated by all maps

\mathbb{D}([0,T],U^{\prime})\ni x\mapsto x(s)\in U^{\prime},\,s\leq t.

We also set
\mathscr{D}_{t}=\bigcap_{s>t}\mathscr{D}^{0}_{s},\,t\in[0,T]
. Note that
\mathbf{D}=(\mathscr{D}_{t})_{t\in[0,T]}
is a filtration.
Let

h\colon\mathbb{D}([0,T];U^{\prime})\to\mathbb{R}

be a bounded, continuous function and
\mathscr{D}_{s}
-measurable. Let us also choose and fix
\psi\in U
.

Let us first observe that the process
{M}_{n}
is a square integrable,
U^{\prime}
-valued
\mathbb{F}
-martingale. Hence, we have

\mathbb{E}\left[\mathchoice{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle{{M}}_{n}(t)-{{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-80.61581pt\hskip 80.61581pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-80.61581ptV^{\prime}\hskip 80.61581ptV}}}{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle{{M}}_{n}(t)-{{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-80.61581pt\hskip 80.61581pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-80.61581ptV^{\prime}\hskip 80.61581ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle{{M}}_{n}(t)-{{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-63.88388pt\hskip 63.88388pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-63.88388ptV^{\prime}\hskip 63.88388ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle{{M}}_{n}(t)-{{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-57.54683pt\hskip 57.54683pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-57.54683ptV^{\prime}\hskip 57.54683ptV}}}{}\,h\bigl({u}_{n}\bigr)\right]=0

Moreover, thanks to the linearity of
P_{n},\mathscr{A}
and Lemma
5.7
, with
\psi
replaced by
P_{n}\psi
, we infer that the function

\displaystyle I_{s,t,\psi}\colon\mathscr{Z}_{T}\ni z

\displaystyle\mapsto\Bigl(\int_{s}^{t}{P}_{n}\mathscr{A}z(r)\,\mathrm{d}r+\int_{s}^{t}{B}_{n}(z(r))\,\mathrm{d}r

(5.22)

\displaystyle-\int_{s}^{t}{P}_{n}f(r)\,\mathrm{d}r,\psi{\Bigr)}\!{}_{H}\in\mathbb{R}

is measurable.

Thus, since by (
4.5
), the processes
{\overline{u}}_{n}
and
u_{n}
have the same laws on
\mathscr{Z}_{T}
, the laws of the
\mathbb{R}
-valued random variables, see (
5.1
),

\mathchoice{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle{{M}}_{n}(t)-{{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-80.61581pt\hskip 80.61581pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-80.61581ptV^{\prime}\hskip 80.61581ptV}}}{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle{{M}}_{n}(t)-{{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-80.61581pt\hskip 80.61581pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-80.61581ptV^{\prime}\hskip 80.61581ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle{{M}}_{n}(t)-{{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-63.88388pt\hskip 63.88388pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-63.88388ptV^{\prime}\hskip 63.88388ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle{{M}}_{n}(t)-{{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-57.54683pt\hskip 57.54683pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-57.54683ptV^{\prime}\hskip 57.54683ptV}}}{}\,h\bigl({u}_{n}\bigr)=I_{s,t,\psi}({u}_{n})h\bigl({u}_{n}\bigr)

and, see (
5.8
),

\mathchoice{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle\overline{M}_{n}(t)-\overline{M}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-69.03253pt\hskip 69.03253pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-69.03253ptV^{\prime}\hskip 69.03253ptV}}}{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle\overline{M}_{n}(t)-\overline{M}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-69.03253pt\hskip 69.03253pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-69.03253ptV^{\prime}\hskip 69.03253ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle\overline{M}_{n}(t)-\overline{M}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-57.1311pt\hskip 57.1311pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-57.1311ptV^{\prime}\hskip 57.1311ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle\overline{M}_{n}(t)-\overline{M}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-53.6162pt\hskip 53.6162pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-53.6162ptV^{\prime}\hskip 53.6162ptV}}}{}\,h\bigl({\overline{u}}_{n}\bigr)=I_{s,t,\psi}({\overline{u}}_{n})h\bigl({{\overline{u}}_{n}}\bigr)

are equal.
Therefore, we infer that

\mathbb{E}\biggl[\mathchoice{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle{{M}}_{n}(t)-{{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-80.61581pt\hskip 80.61581pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-80.61581ptV^{\prime}\hskip 80.61581ptV}}}{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle{{M}}_{n}(t)-{{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-80.61581pt\hskip 80.61581pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-80.61581ptV^{\prime}\hskip 80.61581ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle{{M}}_{n}(t)-{{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-63.88388pt\hskip 63.88388pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-63.88388ptV^{\prime}\hskip 63.88388ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle{{M}}_{n}(t)-{{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-57.54683pt\hskip 57.54683pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-57.54683ptV^{\prime}\hskip 57.54683ptV}}}{}\,h\bigl({u}_{n}\bigr)\biggr]=\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathchoice{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle\overline{M}_{n}(t)-\overline{M}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-69.03253pt\hskip 69.03253pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-69.03253ptV^{\prime}\hskip 69.03253ptV}}}{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle\overline{M}_{n}(t)-\overline{M}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-69.03253pt\hskip 69.03253pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-69.03253ptV^{\prime}\hskip 69.03253ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle\overline{M}_{n}(t)-\overline{M}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-57.1311pt\hskip 57.1311pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-57.1311ptV^{\prime}\hskip 57.1311ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle\overline{M}_{n}(t)-\overline{M}_{n}(s),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-53.6162pt\hskip 53.6162pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-53.6162ptV^{\prime}\hskip 53.6162ptV}}}{}\,h\bigl({\overline{u}}_{n}\bigr)\right]=0.

(5.23)

With this at hand, by using standard Functional Monotone Class argument as in
[
35
, Proposition IX.1.1]
we see that for any
s,t\in[0,T]
with
s\leq t
and
h\colon\mathbb{D}([0,T];U^{\prime})\to\mathbb{R}
bounded and
\mathscr{D}_{s}
-measurable we have

\overline{\mathbb{E}}\bigl[\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}_{n}(t)-\overline{M}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-69.03253pt\hskip 69.03253pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-69.03253ptU^{\prime}\hskip 69.03253ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}_{n}(t)-\overline{M}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-69.03253pt\hskip 69.03253pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-69.03253ptU^{\prime}\hskip 69.03253ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}_{n}(t)-\overline{M}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-57.1311pt\hskip 57.1311pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-57.1311ptU^{\prime}\hskip 57.1311ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}_{n}(t)-\overline{M}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-53.6162pt\hskip 53.6162pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-53.6162ptU^{\prime}\hskip 53.6162ptU}}}{}\,h\bigl(\overline{u}\bigr)\bigr]=0,

which implies that
\overline{M}_{n}
is a
\overline{\mathscr{F}}
-martingale with respect to the filtration generated by
\overline{u}_{n}
. By Lemma
E.2
it is a martingale with respect to the filtration
\overline{\mathbb{F}}_{n}
.

If
p\in[2,4+\gamma]
, then thanks to the measurability of (
4.18
), the random variables
\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\lvert M_{n}(t)\rvert^{p}_{U^{\prime}}
and
\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\lvert\overline{M}_{n}(t)\rvert^{p}_{U^{\prime}}
, have the same law.
Therefore, by the first part of Lemma
5.1
in the case when
p=2
, and by Assumption F.
5
and the second part of Lemma
5.1
in the case when
p\in(2,4+\gamma]
, we infer that

\sup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\lvert\overline{M}_{n}(t)\rvert^{p}_{U^{\prime}}\right]=\sup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\lvert M_{n}(t)\rvert^{p}_{U^{\prime}}\right]<\infty.

(5.24)

Hence, the proof of Lemma
5.8
is complete.
∎

The above proof of Lemma
5.8
relies on the fact that the original process
M_{n}
is a martingale on the original filtered probability space

\bigl({\Omega},\mathscr{F},{\mathbb{P}},{\mathbb{F}}\bigr)
, where
{\mathbb{F}}=(\mathscr{F}_{t})_{t\geq 0}
.
Similarly, the proof of the following Lemma
will rely on the fact that the original process
N_{n,\phi}
defined by (
5.2
) is a martingale on the original filtered probability space, which will be justified using the Itô formula.

In the following Lemma we use
estimate (
4.3
)
with
p=4
, which was proven under linear growth condition (
3.7
) in Assumption F.
5
with
p=4
.

Lemma 5.9
.

Assume that
\phi\in U
.
Then the process
\overline{N}_{n,\phi}
is a square integrable
\mathbb{R}
-valued
{\overline{\mathbb{F}}}_{n}
-martingale.

Proof.

Let
\phi\in U
and
n\in\mathbb{N}
.
Applying the Itô formula
[
34
, Theorem II.5.1]
, see also Theorem
A.1
,
to the real semimartingale
X_{n}=\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{u}_{n},\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.34846pt\hskip 29.34846pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-29.34846ptU^{\prime}\hskip 29.34846ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{u}_{n},\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.34846pt\hskip 29.34846pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-29.34846ptU^{\prime}\hskip 29.34846ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{u}_{n},\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-24.48209pt\hskip 24.48209pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-24.48209ptU^{\prime}\hskip 24.48209ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{u}_{n},\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-23.13448pt\hskip 23.13448pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-23.13448ptU^{\prime}\hskip 23.13448ptU}}}
, where the process
{u}_{n}
is given by (
4.1
), we get for
t\in[0,T]

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\langle u_{n}(t),\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.73738pt\hskip 40.73738pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-40.73738ptU^{\prime}\hskip 40.73738ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\langle u_{n}(t),\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.73738pt\hskip 40.73738pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-40.73738ptU^{\prime}\hskip 40.73738ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\langle u_{n}(t),\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-33.75296pt\hskip 33.75296pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-33.75296ptU^{\prime}\hskip 33.75296ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\langle u_{n}(t),\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-31.3695pt\hskip 31.3695pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-31.3695ptU^{\prime}\hskip 31.3695ptU}}}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\langle P_{n}u_{0},\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.64363pt\hskip 41.64363pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-41.64363ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.64363ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\langle P_{n}u_{0},\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.64363pt\hskip 41.64363pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-41.64363ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.64363ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\langle P_{n}u_{0},\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.55263pt\hskip 34.55263pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.55263ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.55263ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\langle P_{n}u_{0},\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-32.28215pt\hskip 32.28215pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-32.28215ptU^{\prime}\hskip 32.28215ptU}}}-2\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}\mathscr{A}u_{n}(s)+B_{n}(u_{n}(s)),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-119.72943pt\hskip 119.72943pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-119.72943ptU^{\prime}\hskip 119.72943ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}\mathscr{A}u_{n}(s)+B_{n}(u_{n}(s)),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-119.72943pt\hskip 119.72943pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-119.72943ptU^{\prime}\hskip 119.72943ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}\mathscr{A}u_{n}(s)+B_{n}(u_{n}(s)),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-94.8133pt\hskip 94.8133pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-94.8133ptU^{\prime}\hskip 94.8133ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}\mathscr{A}u_{n}(s)+B_{n}(u_{n}(s)),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-85.73358pt\hskip 85.73358pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-85.73358ptU^{\prime}\hskip 85.73358ptU}}}\;\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle u_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.81377pt\hskip 41.81377pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-41.81377ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.81377ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle u_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.81377pt\hskip 41.81377pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-41.81377ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.81377ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle u_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.50644pt\hskip 34.50644pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.50644ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.50644ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle u_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-31.90192pt\hskip 31.90192pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-31.90192ptU^{\prime}\hskip 31.90192ptU}}}\,\mathrm{d}s

(5.25)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Y}\langle{P}_{n}F(s,{u}_{n}(s),y),\phi\rangle^{2}\,\nu(\mathrm{d}y)\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+2\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Y}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle u_{n}(s-),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-47.36926pt\hskip 47.36926pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-47.36926ptU^{\prime}\hskip 47.36926ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle u_{n}(s-),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-47.36926pt\hskip 47.36926pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-47.36926ptU^{\prime}\hskip 47.36926ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle u_{n}(s-),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-37.2009pt\hskip 37.2009pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-37.2009ptU^{\prime}\hskip 37.2009ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle u_{n}(s-),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.26306pt\hskip 34.26306pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.26306ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.26306ptU}}}\;\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}F(s,{u}_{n}(s-),y),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-93.39018pt\hskip 93.39018pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-93.39018ptU^{\prime}\hskip 93.39018ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}F(s,{u}_{n}(s-),y),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-93.39018pt\hskip 93.39018pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-93.39018ptU^{\prime}\hskip 93.39018ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}F(s,{u}_{n}(s-),y),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-73.27945pt\hskip 73.27945pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-73.27945ptU^{\prime}\hskip 73.27945ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}F(s,{u}_{n}(s-),y),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-66.2443pt\hskip 66.2443pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-66.2443ptU^{\prime}\hskip 66.2443ptU}}}\,\tilde{\eta}(\mathrm{d}s,\mathrm{d}y).

Using the definition (
5.2
) of the process
N_{n,\phi}
it follows that equality (
5.25
) can be rewritten in the following way

\displaystyle{N}_{n,\phi}(t)=2\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Y}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle u_{n}(s-),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-47.36926pt\hskip 47.36926pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-47.36926ptU^{\prime}\hskip 47.36926ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle u_{n}(s-),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-47.36926pt\hskip 47.36926pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-47.36926ptU^{\prime}\hskip 47.36926ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle u_{n}(s-),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-37.2009pt\hskip 37.2009pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-37.2009ptU^{\prime}\hskip 37.2009ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle u_{n}(s-),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.26306pt\hskip 34.26306pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.26306ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.26306ptU}}}\;\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}F(s,{u}_{n}(s-),y),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-93.39018pt\hskip 93.39018pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-93.39018ptU^{\prime}\hskip 93.39018ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}F(s,{u}_{n}(s-),y),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-93.39018pt\hskip 93.39018pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-93.39018ptU^{\prime}\hskip 93.39018ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}F(s,{u}_{n}(s-),y),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-73.27945pt\hskip 73.27945pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-73.27945ptU^{\prime}\hskip 73.27945ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}F(s,{u}_{n}(s-),y),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-66.2443pt\hskip 66.2443pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-66.2443ptU^{\prime}\hskip 66.2443ptU}}}\,\tilde{\eta}(\mathrm{d}s,\mathrm{d}y),\;\;\;t\in[0,T].

From the linear growth condition (
3.5
) in Assumption F.
2
along with the estimate (
4.3
)
with
p=4

we infer that the process defined on the right hand side of the above equation is a square integrable
\mathbb{F}
-martingale, see
[
15
, Section 3]
.
This completes the proof of the fact that
{N}_{n,\phi}
is a square integrable
\mathbb{F}
-martingale.

With the above result at hand we can closely follow the proof of Lemma
5.8
and show that
\overline{N}_{n,\phi}
is a square integrable
\overline{\mathbb{F}}_{n}
-martingale.
∎

Let us recall that according to Remark
4.6

the
\mathscr{Z}_{T}
-valued random variables
\overline{u}
defined on
\bigl(\overline{\Omega},\overline{\mathscr{F}},\overline{\mathbb{P}}\bigr)
induces
a measurable process (denoted by the same letter)

\overline{u}\colon[0,T]\times\overline{\Omega}\to V\cap{H}_{\mathrm{loc}},

whose trajectories belong to the space
\mathscr{Z}_{T}
.

Let us introduce the following limiting filtration

\displaystyle{\overline{\mathbb{F}}}

\displaystyle\coloneqq({\overline{\mathscr{F}}}_{t})_{t\in[0,T]},\mbox{ where }{\overline{\mathscr{F}}}_{t}=\sigma\bigl(\mathscr{H}_{t+}\cup\mathscr{N}\bigr)

(5.26)

\displaystyle\mbox{ and }\mathscr{H}_{t+}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigcap_{s>t}\mathscr{H}_{s}\mbox{ with }\mathscr{H}_{s}\coloneqq\sigma\{\overline{u}(r),\,\,r\leq s\}.

Let us define an
U^{\prime}
-valued process
{\overline{M}}
by the following formula (on full
\overline{\Omega}
)

\displaystyle{\overline{M}}(t)

\displaystyle=\overline{u}(t)\,-\overline{u}(0)-\int_{0}^{t}f(s)\,\mathrm{d}s

(5.27)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(s)\,\mathrm{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}{B}\bigl(\overline{u}(s)\bigr)\,\mathrm{d}s,\quad t\in[0,T].

Also, given
\phi\in U
let us define, also on full
\overline{\Omega}
, a
\mathbb{R}
-valued process
\overline{N}_{\phi}
by

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \overline{N}_{\phi}(t)&=\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\langle\overline{u}(t),\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.56949pt\hskip 34.56949pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-34.56949ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.56949ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\langle\overline{u}(t),\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.56949pt\hskip 34.56949pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-34.56949ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.56949ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\langle\overline{u}(t),\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.12093pt\hskip 29.12093pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-29.12093ptU^{\prime}\hskip 29.12093ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\langle\overline{u}(t),\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.23514pt\hskip 27.23514pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-27.23514ptU^{\prime}\hskip 27.23514ptU}}}-\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\langle u_{0},\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-28.39127pt\hskip 28.39127pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-28.39127ptU^{\prime}\hskip 28.39127ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\langle u_{0},\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-28.39127pt\hskip 28.39127pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-28.39127ptU^{\prime}\hskip 28.39127ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\langle u_{0},\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-23.48093pt\hskip 23.48093pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-23.48093ptU^{\prime}\hskip 23.48093ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\langle u_{0},\phi\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-22.13332pt\hskip 22.13332pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-22.13332ptU^{\prime}\hskip 22.13332ptU}}}\\
&+2\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(s)+B(\overline{u}(s))-f(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-114.91318pt\hskip 114.91318pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-114.91318ptU^{\prime}\hskip 114.91318ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(s)+B(\overline{u}(s))-f(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-114.91318pt\hskip 114.91318pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-114.91318ptU^{\prime}\hskip 114.91318ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(s)+B(\overline{u}(s))-f(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-86.97643pt\hskip 86.97643pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-86.97643ptU^{\prime}\hskip 86.97643ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(s)+B(\overline{u}(s))-f(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-77.55106pt\hskip 77.55106pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-77.55106ptU^{\prime}\hskip 77.55106ptU}}}\;\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.64587pt\hskip 35.64587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-35.64587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.64587ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.64587pt\hskip 35.64587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-35.64587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.64587ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.8744pt\hskip 29.8744pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-29.8744ptU^{\prime}\hskip 29.8744ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.76755pt\hskip 27.76755pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-27.76755ptU^{\prime}\hskip 27.76755ptU}}}\,\mathrm{d}s\\
&-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Y}{\bigl(F(s,\overline{u}(s),y),\phi\bigr)}_{H}^{2}\,\nu(\mathrm{d}y)\,\mathrm{d}s,\;\;t\in[0,T].\end{aligned}

(5.28)

We have the following important results.

Lemma 5.10
.

The process
{\overline{M}}
is a square integrable
U^{\prime}
-valued
{\overline{\mathbb{F}}}
-martingale and all its trajectories belong to the space
\mathbb{D}([0,T];U^{\prime})
.

Lemma 5.11
.

Let
\phi\in U
. Then, the process
{\overline{N}_{\phi}}
is a square integrable
{\overline{\mathbb{F}}}
-martingale.

In order to prove Lemmata
5.10
and
5.11
we will need to pass to the limit as
n\to\infty
. For this aim we will need the following auxiliary results.

Lemma 5.12
.

(i)

There exists a set
C\subset[0,T]
such that

(c1)

the complement
[0,T]\setminus C
of
C
is at most countable,

(c2)

\{0,T\}\subset C
,

(c3)

for every
t\in C
,

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}{\overline{u}}_{n}(t)=\overline{u}(t)\mbox{ in }U^{\prime},\;\mbox{ on $\overline{\Omega}$ }.

(5.29)

(ii)

For all
s,t\in[0,T]
with
s\leq t
and every
\psi\in U
, we have on
\overline{\Omega}
,

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{s}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-55.61475pt\hskip 55.61475pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-55.61475ptV^{\prime}\hskip 55.61475ptV}}}{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-55.61475pt\hskip 55.61475pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-55.61475ptV^{\prime}\hskip 55.61475ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-46.92819pt\hskip 46.92819pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-46.92819ptV^{\prime}\hskip 46.92819ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.648pt\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-33.84001pt\hskip (5.30)

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{s}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle B_{n}\bigl({\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-65.34033pt\hskip 65.34033pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-65.34033ptV^{\prime}\hskip 65.34033ptV}}}{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle B_{n}\bigl({\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-65.34033pt\hskip 65.34033pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-65.34033ptV^{\prime}\hskip 65.34033ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle B_{n}\bigl({\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-56.54716pt\hskip 56.54716pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-56.54716ptV^{\prime}\hskip 56.54716ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle B_{n}\bigl({\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-53.30238pt\hskip 53.30238pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-53.30238ptV^{\prime}\hskip 53.30238ptV}}}{}\,\mathrm{d}\sigma=\int_{s}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle B\bigl(\overline{u}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-54.45367pt\hskip 54.45367pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-54.45367ptV^{\prime}\hskip 54.45367ptV}}}{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle B\bigl(\overline{u}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-54.45367pt\hskip 54.45367pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-54.45367ptV^{\prime}\hskip 54.45367ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle B\bigl(\overline{u}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-46.73918pt\hskip
