B\bigl(\overline{u}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.4944pt\hskip 43.4944pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-43.4944ptV^{\prime}\hskip 43.4944ptV}}}{}\,\mathrm{d}\sigma.

(5.31)

Proof of Lemma
5.12
.

By assertion (
4.9
) we infer that
\overline{u}
belongs to
\mathbb{D}([0,T];U^{\prime})
on
\overline{\Omega}
. Thus, by
[
4
, Section 13]
,
the set

C=C(\overline{u})\coloneqq\{t\in(0,T):\overline{\mathbb{P}}(\Delta\overline{u}(t)\not=0)=0\}\cup\{0,T\},

(5.32)

satisfies the condition (c1), i.e. the set
[0,T]\setminus C
is at most countable, and (c2).

Let us choose and fix
t\in C
. Then, since
{\overline{u}}_{n}\to\overline{u}
in
\mathbb{D}([0,T];U^{\prime})
on
\overline{\Omega}
, by
[
4
, Th. 12.5(i)]
we infer that

{\overline{u}}_{n}(t)\to\overline{u}(t)\mbox{ in $U^{\prime}$, }\mbox{ on $\overline{\Omega}$ }.

Hence Claim (
5.29
) holds.

We move on to prove assertion (
5.30
).

Let us choose and fix time instants
s,t\in[0,T]
such that
s\leq t
and
\psi\in U
.
Next we observe that without loss of generality we can assume that
s=0
. Let us now choose and fix
\omega\in{\overline{\Omega}}
.
Note that by definitions of
\mathscr{A}_{n}
and
\tilde{P}_{n}
, and equation (
2.38
) we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-55.61475pt\hskip 55.61475pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-55.61475ptV^{\prime}\hskip 55.61475ptV}}}{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-55.61475pt\hskip 55.61475pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-55.61475ptV^{\prime}\hskip 55.61475ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-46.92819pt\hskip 46.92819pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-46.92819ptV^{\prime}\hskip 46.92819ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.648pt\hskip 43.648pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-43.648ptV^{\prime}\hskip 43.648ptV}}}{}=\mathchoice{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\tilde{P}_{n}\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-59.5037pt\hskip 59.5037pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-59.5037ptV^{\prime}\hskip 59.5037ptV}}}{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\tilde{P}_{n}\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-59.5037pt\hskip 59.5037pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-59.5037ptV^{\prime}\hskip 59.5037ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\tilde{P}_{n}\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-52.48376pt\hskip 52.48376pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-52.48376ptV^{\prime}\hskip 52.48376ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\tilde{P}_{n}\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-49.20357pt\hskip 49.20357pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-49.20357ptV^{\prime}\hskip 49.20357ptV}}}{}={\bigl(\nabla{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\nabla\tilde{P}_{n}\psi\bigr)}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}.

Note also that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-55.61475pt\hskip 55.61475pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-55.61475ptV^{\prime}\hskip 55.61475ptV}}}{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-55.61475pt\hskip 55.61475pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-55.61475ptV^{\prime}\hskip 55.61475ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-46.92819pt\hskip 46.92819pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-46.92819ptV^{\prime}\hskip 46.92819ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.648pt\hskip 43.648pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-43.648ptV^{\prime}\hskip 43.648ptV}}}{}\,\mathrm{d}\sigma

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}{\bigl(\nabla{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\nabla\tilde{P}_{n}\psi\bigr)}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}\sigma

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}{\bigl(\nabla{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\nabla\psi\bigr)}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}\sigma

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}{\bigl(\nabla{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\nabla(\tilde{P}_{n}\psi-\psi)\bigr)}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}\sigma

and that by assertion
4.13

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{0}^{t}{\bigl(\nabla{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\nabla\psi\bigr)}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}\sigma=\int_{0}^{t}{\bigl(\nabla\overline{u}(\sigma),\nabla\psi\bigr)}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}\sigma=\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-44.72809pt\hskip 44.72809pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-44.72809ptV^{\prime}\hskip 44.72809ptV}}}{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-44.72809pt\hskip 44.72809pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-44.72809ptV^{\prime}\hskip 44.72809ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-37.12021pt\hskip 37.12021pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-37.12021ptV^{\prime}\hskip 37.12021ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(\sigma),\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-33.84001pt\hskip 33.84001pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-33.84001ptV^{\prime}\hskip 33.84001ptV}}}{}\,\mathrm{d}\sigma.

Moreover, since on the one hand, by Lemma
2.6
,
\tilde{P}_{n}\psi\to\psi
in
U
and hence, by the second part of Remark
2.7
, also in
V

and, on the other hand, by inequality (
4.2
) in Lemma
4.2

the sequence
(\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n})_{n=1}^{\infty}
is bounded in
L^{2}(0,T;V^{\prime})
, we infer that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}{\bigl(\nabla{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\nabla(\tilde{P}_{n}\psi-\psi)\bigr)}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}\sigma

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\tilde{P}_{n}\psi-\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-73.79527pt\hskip 73.79527pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-73.79527ptV^{\prime}\hskip 73.79527ptV}}}{{\hskip 9.14935pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\tilde{P}_{n}\psi-\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-73.79527pt\hskip 73.79527pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-9.14935pt\hskip-73.79527ptV^{\prime}\hskip 73.79527ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\tilde{P}_{n}\psi-\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-60.46992pt\hskip 60.46992pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-60.46992ptV^{\prime}\hskip 60.46992ptV}}}{{\hskip 8.23274pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\big\langle\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\tilde{P}_{n}\psi-\psi\big\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-56.21756pt\hskip 56.21756pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-8.23274pt\hskip-56.21756ptV^{\prime}\hskip 56.21756ptV}}}{}\,\mathrm{d}\sigma\to 0.

Hence the assertion (
5.30
) follows.
∎

Now we prove (
5.31
).

Since by (
4.6
), as in the proof of (
5.30
),
{\overline{u}}_{n}\to\overline{u}

in
{L}^{2}_{w}(0,T;V)
, we infer that
\overline{u}\in{L}^{2}(0,T;V)
on
\overline{\Omega}
and the sequence

({\overline{u}}_{n}(\cdot,\omega){)}_{n=1}^{\infty}
is bounded in
{L}^{2}(0,T;V)
for all
\omega\in\overline{\Omega}
.
Thus for all
\omega\in\overline{\Omega}
.
\overline{u}\in{L}^{2}(0,T;{H})
and the sequence
({\overline{u}}_{n}{)}_{n=1}^{\infty}
is bounded in
{L}^{2}(0,T;{H})
, as well.

By (
4.10
) the sequence
({\overline{u}}_{n}{)}_{n\geq 1}
is bounded in
B_{b}([0,T];H)
, i.e. we can find
N=N(\omega)\in\mathbb{N}
such that

\sup_{n\geq 1}\;\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\;\;\left\lVert{\overline{u}}_{n}(t)\right\rVert_{H}{}\leq N.

(5.33)

Thus, if
n\geq N
then
{\chi}_{n}({\overline{u}}_{n})={\overline{u}}_{n}
and consequently

B_{n}\bigl({\overline{u}}_{n},{\overline{u}}_{n}\bigr)=P_{n}B\bigl({\chi}_{n}({\overline{u}}_{n}),{\overline{u}}_{n}\bigr)=P_{n}B\bigl({\overline{u}}_{n},{\overline{u}}_{n}\bigr).

Hence assertion (
5.31
) follows from
[
17
, Cor. B.2]
because
{\overline{u}}_{n}(\cdot,\omega)\to\overline{u}(\cdot,\omega)
in
{L}^{2}(0,T,{H}_{\mathrm{loc}})
by
(
4.7
) and the sequence
({\overline{u}}_{n}(\cdot,\omega){)}_{n\geq 1}
is bounded in
{L}^{2}(0,T;{H})
by (
4.10
). This completes the proof of assertion (
5.31
).
∎

Since all assertions are proved, the proof of Lemma
5.12
is complete.
∎

Remark 5.13
.

Let us note that it follows from equality (
5.29
) in Lemma
5.12
that for every
t\in C
and every
\psi\in U

\lim_{n\to\infty}{\bigl({\overline{u}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr)}_{H}={\bigl(\overline{u}(t),\psi\bigr)}_{H},\quad\overline{\mathbb{P}}\mbox{ - a.s.}

We now claim that the above convergence also holds for all
\psi\in H
.

Indeed, let us fix
\psi\in H
. Since
U
is dense in H, we infer that for every
\varepsilon>0
there exists
{\psi}_{\varepsilon}\in U
such that
|\psi-{\psi}_{\varepsilon}|_{H}<\varepsilon
.
We have

({\bar{u}}_{n}(t)-\bar{u}(t),\psi)_{H}=({\bar{u}}_{n}(t)-\bar{u}(t),\psi-{\psi}_{\varepsilon})_{H}+({\bar{u}}_{n}(t)-\bar{u}(t),{\psi}_{\varepsilon})_{H}

and hence

|({\bar{u}}_{n}(t)-\bar{u}(t),\psi)_{H}|\leq(|{\bar{u}}_{n}(t)|_{H}+|\bar{u}(t)|_{H})\cdot|\psi-{\psi}_{\varepsilon}|_{H}+|({\bar{u}}_{n}(t)-\bar{u}(t),{\psi}_{\varepsilon})_{H}|.

By (
4.10
) we can find
K=K(\omega)
such that

\sup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\bigl(\sup_{t\in[0,T]}|{\bar{u}}_{n}(t)|_{H}+\sup_{t\in[0,T]}|\bar{u}(t)|_{H}\bigr)\leq K.

Thus

|({\bar{u}}_{n}(t)-\bar{u}(t),\psi)_{H}|\leq\varepsilon K+|({\bar{u}}_{n}(t)-\bar{u}(t),{\psi}_{\varepsilon})_{H}|.

Passing to the upper limit as
n\to\infty
and using
\lim_{n\to\infty}({\bar{u}}_{n}(t)-\bar{u}(t),{\psi}_{\varepsilon})_{H}=0
, we obtain

\limsup_{n\to\infty}|({\bar{u}}_{n}(t)-\bar{u}(t),\psi)_{H}|\leq\varepsilon K.

Since
\varepsilon
is arbitrary, we infer that

\lim_{n\to\infty}({\bar{u}}_{n}(t)-\bar{u}(t),\psi)_{H}=0,

which completes the proof of the claim.
∎

Let us also emphasize that the set
C
is chosen according to the process
\overline{u}
being considered as
U^{\prime}
-valued càdlàg process.

Let us also observe that both the left-hand sides of the equalities (
5.30
) and (
5.31
) in Lemma
5.12
make sense as the
{H}
-scalar product.
However, the right-hand sides of (
5.30
) and (
5.31
) make sense only as the
V^{\prime}-V
(or
U^{\prime}-U
) duality. Moreover,
in order to prove these two identities we need to pass to the limit and for this purpose we use the
V^{\prime}-V
-duality and we have to consider
\psi\in U
, see Remark

2.7
for an explanation why taking
\psi\in V
would not work.

This and the previous ones apply as well to Lemma
5.14
below.

We now give the promised proof of Lemma
5.10
.

Proof of Lemma
5.10
.

Let us first observe that in view of the paths properties of the process
\overline{u}
, all trajectories of the process
{\overline{M}}

belong to the space
\mathbb{D}([0,T];U^{\prime})
.

The rest of the proof will be divided into three steps.

Step 1
.

The aim of this step is to prove the square integrability of
\overline{M}
. For this purpose let us fix an arbitrary time instant
t\in C
, where
C
is the set from the first part of Lemma
5.12
.
Then, by (
5.8
) we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.56839pt\hskip 40.56839pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-40.56839ptU^{\prime}\hskip 40.56839ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.56839pt\hskip 40.56839pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-40.56839ptU^{\prime}\hskip 40.56839ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.50829pt\hskip 34.50829pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.50829ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.50829ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-32.5836pt\hskip 32.5836pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-32.5836ptU^{\prime}\hskip 32.5836ptU}}}{}=

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.56839pt\hskip 40.56839pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-40.56839ptU^{\prime}\hskip 40.56839ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.56839pt\hskip 40.56839pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-40.56839ptU^{\prime}\hskip 40.56839ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.50829pt\hskip 34.50829pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.50829ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.50829ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-32.5836pt\hskip 32.5836pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-32.5836ptU^{\prime}\hskip 32.5836ptU}}}-\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}\bar{u}(0),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-49.76633pt\hskip 49.76633pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-49.76633ptU^{\prime}\hskip 49.76633ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}\bar{u}(0),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-49.76633pt\hskip 49.76633pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-49.76633ptU^{\prime}\hskip 49.76633ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}\bar{u}(0),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.64128pt\hskip 41.64128pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-41.64128ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.64128ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle P_{n}\bar{u}(0),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-38.41296pt\hskip 38.41296pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-38.41296ptU^{\prime}\hskip 38.41296ptU}}}+\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-63.42378pt\hskip 63.42378pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-63.42378ptU^{\prime}\hskip 63.42378ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-63.42378pt\hskip 63.42378pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-63.42378ptU^{\prime}\hskip 63.42378ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-53.0959pt\hskip 53.0959pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-53.0959ptU^{\prime}\hskip 53.0959ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-48.89284pt\hskip 48.89284pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-48.89284ptU^{\prime}\hskip 48.89284ptU}}}{}\mathrm{d}\sigma

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}B\bigl({\chi}_{n}({\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)),{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-119.34047pt\hskip 119.34047pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-119.34047ptU^{\prime}\hskip 119.34047ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}B\bigl({\chi}_{n}({\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)),{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-119.34047pt\hskip 119.34047pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-119.34047ptU^{\prime}\hskip 119.34047ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}B\bigl({\chi}_{n}({\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)),{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-101.91731pt\hskip 101.91731pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-101.91731ptU^{\prime}\hskip 101.91731ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}B\bigl({\chi}_{n}({\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)),{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-94.6014pt\hskip 94.6014pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-94.6014ptU^{\prime}\hskip 94.6014ptU}}}{}\mathrm{d}\sigma

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}f(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-51.45268pt\hskip 51.45268pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-51.45268ptU^{\prime}\hskip 51.45268ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}f(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-51.45268pt\hskip 51.45268pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-51.45268ptU^{\prime}\hskip 51.45268ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}f(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.97316pt\hskip 41.97316pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-41.97316ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.97316ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}f(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-38.09642pt\hskip 38.09642pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-38.09642ptU^{\prime}\hskip 38.09642ptU}}}{}\mathrm{d}\sigma.

By Lemma
5.12
, we infer that

\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.56839pt\hskip 40.56839pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-40.56839ptU^{\prime}\hskip 40.56839ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.56839pt\hskip 40.56839pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-40.56839ptU^{\prime}\hskip 40.56839ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.50829pt\hskip 34.50829pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.50829ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.50829ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-32.5836pt\hskip 32.5836pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-32.5836ptU^{\prime}\hskip 32.5836ptU}}}{}=\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.12506pt\hskip 35.12506pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-35.12506ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.12506ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.12506pt\hskip 35.12506pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-35.12506ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.12506ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.6043pt\hskip 29.6043pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-29.6043ptU^{\prime}\hskip 29.6043ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.67961pt\hskip 27.67961pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-27.67961ptU^{\prime}\hskip 27.67961ptU}}}{},\quad\overline{\mathbb{P}}\mbox{ - a.s.},

which clearly implies that
\overline{\mathbb{P}}
-a.s.

\overline{M}_{n}(t)\to\overline{M}(t)\mbox{ weak}^{\ast}\mbox{ in }U^{\prime}.

The latter assertion implies that

\lvert\overline{M}(t)\rvert_{U^{\prime}}^{2}\leq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\lvert\overline{M}_{n}(t)\rvert_{U^{\prime}}^{2},\;\;\mbox{ $\overline{\mathbb{P}}$-a.s.}.

By applying the Fatou Lemma we see that

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lvert\overline{M}(t)\rvert^{2}_{U^{\prime}}\right]\leq\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\liminf_{n\to\infty}\lvert\overline{M}_{n}(t)\rvert_{U^{\prime}}^{2}\right]\leq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lvert\overline{M}_{n}(t)\rvert_{U^{\prime}}^{2}\right],

which implies that

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lvert\overline{M}(t)\rvert^{2}_{U^{\prime}}\right]\leq\sup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\sup_{r\in[0,T]}\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lvert\overline{M}_{n}(r)\rvert_{U^{\prime}}^{2}\right]=\sup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\sup_{r\in[0,T]}\mathbb{E}\left[\lvert M_{n}(r)\rvert^{2}_{U^{\prime}}\right]<\infty

(5.34)

where the last equality is due to (
5.24
) and the finiteness is due to Lemma
5.1
.
Thus we infer that

\sup_{r\in C}\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lvert\overline{M}(r)\rvert_{U^{\prime}}^{2}\right]\leq\sup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\sup_{s\in[0,T]}\mathbb{E}\left[\lvert M_{n}(s)\rvert_{U^{\prime}}^{2}\right]<\infty,

(5.35)

which shows that
\overline{M}(t)
is square integrable for all
t\in C
.

Our next goal is to show that
\overline{M}(t)
is square integrable for every
t\in[0,T]
. In order to achieve this aim we fix an arbitrary
t\in[0,T]
and consider a
C
-valued sequence
(t_{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}}
such that
t_{m}\searrow t
as
m\to\infty
.
With this in mind, we deduce from the right-continuity of the paths of
\overline{M}
that
\overline{\mathbb{P}}
-a.s.

\overline{M}(t_{m})\to\overline{M}(t)\mbox{ in }U^{\prime}.

Thus, similarly to the proof of assertion (
5.34
) we can prove that

\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lvert\overline{M}(t)\rvert^{2}_{U^{\prime}}\right]\leq\sup_{m\in\mathbb{N}}\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lvert\overline{M}(t_{m})\rvert^{2}_{U^{\prime}}\right].

With this inequality at hand and (
5.35
) we now easily conclude that for all
t\in[0,T]

we have

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lvert\overline{M}(t)\rvert^{2}_{U^{\prime}}\right]<\infty.

This completes the proof in Step 1.
∎

Step 2
.

Following
[
35
]
we denote by
\mathscr{D}_{t}^{0}
the
\sigma
-algebra generated by all maps

\mathbb{D}([0,T],U^{\prime})\ni x\mapsto x(s)\in U^{\prime},\,s\leq t.

We also set
\mathscr{D}_{t}=\bigcap_{s>t}\mathscr{D}^{0}_{s},\,t\in[0,T]
. Note that
\mathbf{D}=(\mathscr{D}_{t})_{t\in[0,T]}
is a filtration.
Let
C
be the set from the assertion (a) of Lemma
5.12
.
Let us choose and fix
s,t\in C
, with
s\leq t
, and
\psi\in U
. Let us note that the almost sure set considered below is independent of
\psi
. Let

h\colon\mathbb{D}([0,T];U^{\prime})\to\mathbb{R}

be a bounded, continuous function and
\mathscr{D}_{s}
-measurable.
The aim of this step is to show that

\overline{\mathbb{E}}\bigl[\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-58.14587pt\hskip 58.14587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-58.14587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 58.14587ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-58.14587pt\hskip 58.14587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-58.14587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 58.14587ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-47.32312pt\hskip 47.32312pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-47.32312ptU^{\prime}\hskip 47.32312ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.80821pt\hskip 43.80821pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-43.80821ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.80821ptU}}}{}\,h\bigl(\overline{u}\bigr)\bigr]=0.

(5.36)

For this purpose, we start observing that by (
5.8
) we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t)-{\overline{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-69.03253pt\hskip 69.03253pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-69.03253ptU^{\prime}\hskip 69.03253ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t)-{\overline{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-69.03253pt\hskip 69.03253pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-69.03253ptU^{\prime}\hskip 69.03253ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t)-{\overline{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-57.1311pt\hskip 57.1311pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-57.1311ptU^{\prime}\hskip 57.1311ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t)-{\overline{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-53.6162pt\hskip 53.6162pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-53.6162ptU^{\prime}\hskip 53.6162ptU}}}{}

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.56839pt\hskip 40.56839pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-40.56839ptU^{\prime}\hskip 40.56839ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.56839pt\hskip 40.56839pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-40.56839ptU^{\prime}\hskip 40.56839ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.50829pt\hskip 34.50829pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.50829ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.50829ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-32.5836pt\hskip 32.5836pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-32.5836ptU^{\prime}\hskip 32.5836ptU}}}-\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.64478pt\hskip 41.64478pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-41.64478ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.64478ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.64478pt\hskip 41.64478pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-41.64478ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.64478ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.26176pt\hskip 35.26176pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-35.26176ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.26176ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-33.11601pt\hskip 33.11601pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-33.11601ptU^{\prime}\hskip 33.11601ptU}}}+\int_{s}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-63.42378pt\hskip 63.42378pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-63.42378ptU^{\prime}\hskip 63.42378ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-63.42378pt\hskip 63.42378pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-63.42378ptU^{\prime}\hskip 63.42378ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-53.0959pt\hskip 53.0959pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-53.0959ptU^{\prime}\hskip 53.0959ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-48.89284pt\hskip 48.89284pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-48.89284ptU^{\prime}\hskip 48.89284ptU}}}{}\,\mathrm{d}\sigma

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\int_{s}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}B\bigl({\chi}_{n}({\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)),{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-119.34047pt\hskip 119.34047pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-119.34047ptU^{\prime}\hskip 119.34047ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}B\bigl({\chi}_{n}({\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)),{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-119.34047pt\hskip 119.34047pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-119.34047ptU^{\prime}\hskip 119.34047ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}B\bigl({\chi}_{n}({\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)),{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-101.91731pt\hskip 101.91731pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-101.91731ptU^{\prime}\hskip 101.91731ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}B\bigl({\chi}_{n}({\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)),{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-94.6014pt\hskip 94.6014pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-94.6014ptU^{\prime}\hskip 94.6014ptU}}}{}\,\mathrm{d}\sigma-\int_{s}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}f(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-51.45268pt\hskip 51.45268pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-51.45268ptU^{\prime}\hskip 51.45268ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}f(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-51.45268pt\hskip 51.45268pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-51.45268ptU^{\prime}\hskip 51.45268ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}f(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.97316pt\hskip 41.97316pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-41.97316ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.97316ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{P}_{n}f(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-38.09642pt\hskip 38.09642pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-38.09642ptU^{\prime}\hskip 38.09642ptU}}}{}\,\mathrm{d}\sigma.

By Lemma
5.12
, we infer that

\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t)-{\overline{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-69.03253pt\hskip 69.03253pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-69.03253ptU^{\prime}\hskip 69.03253ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t)-{\overline{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-69.03253pt\hskip 69.03253pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-69.03253ptU^{\prime}\hskip 69.03253ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t)-{\overline{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-57.1311pt\hskip 57.1311pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-57.1311ptU^{\prime}\hskip 57.1311ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t)-{\overline{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-53.6162pt\hskip 53.6162pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-53.6162ptU^{\prime}\hskip 53.6162ptU}}}{}=\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-58.14587pt\hskip 58.14587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-58.14587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 58.14587ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-58.14587pt\hskip 58.14587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-58.14587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 58.14587ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-47.32312pt\hskip 47.32312pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-47.32312ptU^{\prime}\hskip 47.32312ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.80821pt\hskip 43.80821pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-43.80821ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.80821ptU}}}{},\quad\overline{\mathbb{P}}\mbox{ - a.s.}

(5.37)

Since
{\overline{u}}_{n}\to\overline{u}

\overline{\mathbb{P}}
-a.s. in
\mathbb{D}([0,T];U^{\prime})
and
h
is a continuous function, we infer that

\lim_{n\to\infty}h({\overline{u}}_{n})=h(\overline{u}),\;\;\mbox{$\overline{\mathbb{P}}$-a.s.}.

(5.38)

Let us define a function
f_{n}\colon\overline{\Omega}\to\mathbb{R}
by

{f}_{n}(\omega)\coloneqq\bigl(\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t,\omega),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-51.23734pt\hskip 51.23734pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-51.23734ptU^{\prime}\hskip 51.23734ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t,\omega),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-51.23734pt\hskip 51.23734pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-51.23734ptU^{\prime}\hskip 51.23734ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t,\omega),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.80995pt\hskip 41.80995pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-41.80995ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.80995ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t,\omega),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-39.02928pt\hskip 39.02928pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-39.02928ptU^{\prime}\hskip 39.02928ptU}}}{}-\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(s,\omega),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-52.31372pt\hskip 52.31372pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-52.31372ptU^{\prime}\hskip 52.31372ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(s,\omega),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-52.31372pt\hskip 52.31372pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-52.31372ptU^{\prime}\hskip 52.31372ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(s,\omega),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-42.56343pt\hskip 42.56343pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-42.56343ptU^{\prime}\hskip 42.56343ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(s,\omega),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-39.56169pt\hskip 39.56169pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-39.56169ptU^{\prime}\hskip 39.56169ptU}}}{}\bigr)\,h\bigl({\overline{u}}_{n}\bigr),\qquad\omega\in\overline{\Omega}.

We will prove that the sequence
\{{f}_{n}{\}}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
is uniformly integrable.
For this purpose we claim that

\sup_{n\geq 1}\overline{\mathbb{E}}\bigl[{|{f}_{n}|}^{2}\bigr]<\infty.

(5.39)

Indeed, from the boundedness of the function
h
, the triangle and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequalities we infer that

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{E}}\bigl[{|{f}_{n}|}^{2}\bigr]\leq 2\lvert h\rvert_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\psi\|^{2}_{U}\left(\lVert\overline{M}_{n}(t)\rVert^{2}_{U^{\prime}}+\lVert\overline{M}_{n}(s)\rVert^{2}_{U^{\prime}}\right),

which along with the inequality (
5.20
) imply (
5.39
).

The just proven uniform integrability of the sequence
\{{f}_{n}{\}}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
together with the Vitali Convergence Theorem, the assertions (
5.37
) and (
5.38
), the martingale property of
\overline{M}_{n}
and the
\mathscr{D}_{s}
-measurability of
h
imply that

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\overline{\mathbb{E}}\bigl[\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t)-{\overline{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-69.03253pt\hskip 69.03253pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-69.03253ptU^{\prime}\hskip 69.03253ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t)-{\overline{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-69.03253pt\hskip 69.03253pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-69.03253ptU^{\prime}\hskip 69.03253ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t)-{\overline{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-57.1311pt\hskip 57.1311pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-57.1311ptU^{\prime}\hskip 57.1311ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{M}}_{n}(t)-{\overline{M}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-53.6162pt\hskip 53.6162pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-53.6162ptU^{\prime}\hskip 53.6162ptU}}}{}\,h\bigl({\overline{u}}_{n}\bigr)\bigr]=\overline{\mathbb{E}}\bigl[\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-58.14587pt\hskip 58.14587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-58.14587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 58.14587ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-58.14587pt\hskip 58.14587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-58.14587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 58.14587ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-47.32312pt\hskip 47.32312pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-47.32312ptU^{\prime}\hskip 47.32312ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.80821pt\hskip 43.80821pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-43.80821ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.80821ptU}}}{}\,h\bigl(\overline{u}\bigr)\bigr]=0.

Hence, for all
s,t\in C
with
s\leq t
we have

\overline{\mathbb{E}}\bigl[\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-58.14587pt\hskip 58.14587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-58.14587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 58.14587ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-58.14587pt\hskip 58.14587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-58.14587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 58.14587ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-47.32312pt\hskip 47.32312pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-47.32312ptU^{\prime}\hskip 47.32312ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.80821pt\hskip 43.80821pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-43.80821ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.80821ptU}}}{}\,h\bigl(\overline{u}\bigr)\bigr]=0

∎

Step 3
.

Our aim in this step is to prove the identity

\overline{\mathbb{E}}\bigl[\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-58.14587pt\hskip 58.14587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-58.14587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 58.14587ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-58.14587pt\hskip 58.14587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-58.14587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 58.14587ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-47.32312pt\hskip 47.32312pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-47.32312ptU^{\prime}\hskip 47.32312ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.80821pt\hskip 43.80821pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-43.80821ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.80821ptU}}}{}\,h\bigl(\overline{u}\bigr)\bigr]=0,

for all
t,s\in[0,T]
with
s\leq t
, which will imply that
\overline{M}
is a
\overline{\mathscr{F}}
-martingale.
For this purpose we choose and fix
t,s\in[0,T]
with
s<t
and we consider two
C
-valued sequences
(t_{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}},(s_{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}}
satisfying

•

s<s_{m}<t<t_{m}
for every
m\in\mathbb{N}
,

•

s_{m}\searrow s
and
t_{m}\searrow t
as
m\to\infty
.

We also choose and fix

h\colon\mathbb{D}([0,T];U^{\prime})\to\mathbb{R}

a bounded, continuous function and
\mathscr{D}_{s}
-measurable.
Note that thanks to (
5.36
) we have

\overline{\mathbb{E}}\bigl[\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m})-\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-73.3381pt\hskip 73.3381pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-73.3381ptU^{\prime}\hskip 73.3381ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m})-\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-73.3381pt\hskip 73.3381pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-73.3381ptU^{\prime}\hskip 73.3381ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m})-\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-60.6034pt\hskip 60.6034pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-60.6034ptU^{\prime}\hskip 60.6034ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m})-\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-57.08849pt\hskip 57.08849pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-57.08849ptU^{\prime}\hskip 57.08849ptU}}}{}\,h\bigl(\overline{u}\bigr)\bigr]=0

Thanks to the right-continuity of the paths of the process
\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M},\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-23.73615pt\hskip 23.73615pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-23.73615ptU^{\prime}\hskip 23.73615ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M},\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-23.73615pt\hskip 23.73615pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-23.73615ptU^{\prime}\hskip 23.73615ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M},\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-20.33342pt\hskip 20.33342pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-20.33342ptU^{\prime}\hskip 20.33342ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M},\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-19.44458pt\hskip 19.44458pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-19.44458ptU^{\prime}\hskip 19.44458ptU}}}{}

we have,
\overline{\mathbb{P}}
-a.s.

\displaystyle h(\overline{u})\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.79756pt\hskip 43.79756pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-43.79756ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.79756ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.79756pt\hskip 43.79756pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-43.79756ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.79756ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.99791pt\hskip 36.99791pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-36.99791ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.99791ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.85216pt\hskip 34.85216pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.85216ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.85216ptU}}}{}

\displaystyle\to h(\overline{u})\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.20145pt\hskip 36.20145pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-36.20145ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.20145ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.20145pt\hskip 36.20145pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-36.20145ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.20145ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-30.35777pt\hskip 30.35777pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-30.35777ptU^{\prime}\hskip 30.35777ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-28.21202pt\hskip 28.21202pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-28.21202ptU^{\prime}\hskip 28.21202ptU}}}{},

(5.40)

\displaystyle h(\overline{u})\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-42.72118pt\hskip 42.72118pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-42.72118ptU^{\prime}\hskip 42.72118ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-42.72118pt\hskip 42.72118pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-42.72118ptU^{\prime}\hskip 42.72118ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.24443pt\hskip 36.24443pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-36.24443ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.24443ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.31975pt\hskip 34.31975pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.31975ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.31975ptU}}}{}

\displaystyle\to h(\overline{u})\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.12506pt\hskip 35.12506pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-35.12506ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.12506ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.12506pt\hskip 35.12506pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-35.12506ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.12506ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.6043pt\hskip 29.6043pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-29.6043ptU^{\prime}\hskip 29.6043ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.67961pt\hskip 27.67961pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-27.67961ptU^{\prime}\hskip 27.67961ptU}}}{}.

(5.41)

Also, from (
5.35
), the convergence (
5.37
) and the boundedness of
h
we deduce that

\displaystyle\sup_{r\in C}\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lvert h(\overline{u})\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(r),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.30331pt\hskip 36.30331pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-36.30331ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.30331ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(r),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.30331pt\hskip 36.30331pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-36.30331ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.30331ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(r),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-30.5117pt\hskip 30.5117pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-30.5117ptU^{\prime}\hskip 30.5117ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(r),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-28.39143pt\hskip 28.39143pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-28.39143ptU^{\prime}\hskip 28.39143ptU}}}{}\rvert^{2}\right]<\infty,

which implies that

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in\mathbb{N}}\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lvert h(\overline{u})\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.79756pt\hskip 43.79756pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-43.79756ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.79756ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.79756pt\hskip 43.79756pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-43.79756ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.79756ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.99791pt\hskip 36.99791pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-36.99791ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.99791ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.85216pt\hskip 34.85216pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.85216ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.85216ptU}}}{}\rvert^{2}\right]+\sup_{m\in\mathbb{N}}\overline{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lvert h(\overline{u})\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-42.72118pt\hskip 42.72118pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-42.72118ptU^{\prime}\hskip 42.72118ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-42.72118pt\hskip 42.72118pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-42.72118ptU^{\prime}\hskip 42.72118ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.24443pt\hskip 36.24443pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-36.24443ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.24443ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.31975pt\hskip 34.31975pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.31975ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.31975ptU}}}{}\rvert^{2}\right]<\infty.

Thus, the sequences
\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-42.72118pt\hskip 42.72118pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-42.72118ptU^{\prime}\hskip 42.72118ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-42.72118pt\hskip 42.72118pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-42.72118ptU^{\prime}\hskip 42.72118ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.24443pt\hskip 36.24443pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-36.24443ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.24443ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.31975pt\hskip 34.31975pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.31975ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.31975ptU}}}{}h(\overline{u})
and
\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.79756pt\hskip 43.79756pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-43.79756ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.79756ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.79756pt\hskip 43.79756pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-43.79756ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.79756ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.99791pt\hskip 36.99791pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-36.99791ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.99791ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.85216pt\hskip 34.85216pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.85216ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.85216ptU}}}{}h(\overline{u})
are uniformly integrable which together with (
5.40
), (
5.41
), the Vitali Convergence Theorem and (
5.36
) imply that

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{E}}\bigl[\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-58.14587pt\hskip 58.14587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-58.14587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 58.14587ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-58.14587pt\hskip 58.14587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-58.14587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 58.14587ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-47.32312pt\hskip 47.32312pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-47.32312ptU^{\prime}\hskip 47.32312ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t)-\overline{M}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.80821pt\hskip 43.80821pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-43.80821ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.80821ptU}}}{}\,h\bigl(\overline{u}\bigr)\bigr]=\lim_{m\to\infty}\overline{\mathbb{E}}\bigl[\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m})-\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-73.3381pt\hskip 73.3381pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-73.3381ptU^{\prime}\hskip 73.3381ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m})-\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-73.3381pt\hskip 73.3381pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-73.3381ptU^{\prime}\hskip 73.3381ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m})-\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-60.6034pt\hskip 60.6034pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-60.6034ptU^{\prime}\hskip 60.6034ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{M}(t_{m})-\overline{M}(s_{m}),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-57.08849pt\hskip 57.08849pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-57.08849ptU^{\prime}\hskip 57.08849ptU}}}{}\,h\bigl(\overline{u}\bigr)\bigr]=0.

(5.42)

This completes the proof of
Step 3
. ∎

Having established Steps 1-3, we completed the proof of Lemma
5.10
.
∎

In order to prove Lemmata
5.11
we will need to pass to the limit as
n\to\infty
. For this aim, we will need the following additional auxiliary result, compare with previous Lemma
5.12
.

Lemma 5.14
.

The following assertions hold on
\overline{\Omega}
.

(i)

If
s,t\in[0,T]
such that
s\leq t
and
\phi,\psi\in U
, then

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{s}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-55.61475pt\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.648pt\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-42.11583pt\hskip 42.11583pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-42.11583ptU^{\prime}\hskip 42.11583ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.63802pt\hskip 35.63802pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-35.63802ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.63802ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(\sigma),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-33.39667pt\hskip 33.39667pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-33.39667ptU^{\prime}\hskip 33.39667ptU}}}{}\,\mathrm{d}\sigma

(5.43)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\int_{s}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-44.72809pt\hskip 44.72809pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-44.72809ptU^{\prime}\hskip 44.72809ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-44.72809pt\hskip 44.72809pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-44.72809ptU^{\prime}\hskip 44.72809ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-37.12021pt\hskip 37.12021pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-37.12021ptU^{\prime}\hskip 37.12021ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(\sigma),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-33.84001pt\hskip 33.84001pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-33.84001ptU^{\prime}\hskip 33.84001ptU}}}{}\;\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(\sigma),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.6725pt\hskip 36.6725pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-36.6725ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.6725ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(\sigma),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.6725pt\hskip 36.6725pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-36.6725ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.6725ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(\sigma),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-30.73402pt\hskip (ii)

If
\phi\in{H}
, then in
L^{2}([0,T]\times Y,\Leb\otimes\nu)
,

\displaystyle{\bigl(P_{n}F(r,{\overline{u}}_{n}(r),y),\phi\bigr)}_{H}\to{\bigl(F(r,\overline{u}(r),y),\phi\bigr)}_{H}.

(5.44)

In particular, for every
\phi\in U
,

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{s}^{t}\int_{Y}\left(P_{n}F(r,{\overline{u}}_{n}(r),y),\phi\right)_{H}^{2}\,\nu(\mathrm{d}y)\,\mathrm{d}r

(5.45)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\int_{s}^{t}\int_{Y}\left(F(r,\overline{u}(r),y),\phi\right)_{H}^{2}\,\nu(\mathrm{d}y)\,\mathrm{d}r.

(iii)

Moreover, if

, then

(5.46)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\int_{s}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle B\bigl(\overline{u}(\sigma)\bigr),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip Let us remind that the inner product in the Hilbert space
H
we denote by
{\bigl(\cdot,\cdot\bigr)}_{H}
.

Proof of Lemma
5.14
.

Without loss of generality we can assume that
s=0
. Let us choose and fix

,

and
\omega\in\overline{\Omega}

.

We begin with proving

Indeed, we have

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}{\bigl(\nabla({\overline{u}}_{n}(s)-\overline{u}(s)),\mathbf{1}_{[0,t]}(s){\bigl(\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr)}\nabla\psi\bigr)}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}s

(5.47)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}{\bigl(\nabla{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\nabla\psi\bigr)}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}{\bigl({\overline{u}}_{n}(s)-\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr)}\,\mathrm{d}s=:I_{n}(\phi)+I{\!}I_{n}(\phi)

(5.48)

) to 0 follows from Lemma

in

The proof that the second integral above converges to

consists of two steps. In the first step we take

and then choose

such that

. Then, also because

, we get

\displaystyle I{\!}I_{n}(\phi)

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|\psi|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-16.51382pt\hskip 16.51382pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-16.51382pt\hskip in

what completes the proof that

we have

7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.7784pt\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-32.67154pt\hskip 32.67154ptU}}}{}\,\mathrm{d}s=\int_{0}^{t}{\bigl(\nabla\overline{u}(s),\nabla\psi\bigr)}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}\;\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.64587pt\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.64587pt\hskip 35.64587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-35.64587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.64587ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.8744pt\hskip We conclude the proof by observing that

\displaystyle\biggl|\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-54.58812pt\hskip 54.58812pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-54.58812ptU^{\prime}\hskip 54.58812ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-46.06857pt\hskip 46.06857pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-46.06857ptU^{\prime}\hskip 46.06857ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}_{n}{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-42.92287pt\hskip 42.92287pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-42.92287ptU^{\prime}\hskip 42.92287ptU}}}{}\;\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.0892pt\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.0892pt\hskip 41.0892pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-41.0892ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.0892ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.7784pt\hskip 34.7784pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.7784ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.7784ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-32.67154pt\hskip 32.67154pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-32.67154ptU^{\prime}\hskip 32.67154ptU}}}{}\,\mathrm{d}s-\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.70146pt\hskip 43.70146pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-43.70146ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.70146ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.70146pt\hskip 43.70146pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-43.70146ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.70146ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.26059pt\hskip 36.26059pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-36.26059ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.26059ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-33.11488pt\hskip 33.11488pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-33.11488ptU^{\prime}\hskip 33.11488ptU}}}{}\;\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.64587pt\hskip 35.64587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-35.64587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.64587ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.64587pt\hskip 35.64587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-35.64587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.64587ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.8744pt\hskip 29.8744pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-29.8744ptU^{\prime}\hskip 29.8744ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.76755pt\hskip \displaystyle=\biggl|\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),{P}_{n}\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-60.73053pt\hskip 60.73053pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-60.73053ptU^{\prime}\hskip 60.73053ptU}}}{{\hskip 60.73053pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-60.73053ptU^{\prime}\hskip 60.73053ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),{P}_{n}\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-52.23628pt\hskip 52.23628pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-52.23628ptU^{\prime}\hskip 52.23628ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),{P}_{n}\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-48.16771pt\hskip 48.16771pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-48.16771ptU^{\prime}\hskip 48.16771ptU}}}{}\;\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.0892pt\hskip 41.0892pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-41.0892ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.0892ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.0892pt\hskip 41.0892pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-41.0892ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.0892ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.7784pt\hskip 34.7784pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.7784ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.7784ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-32.67154pt\hskip 32.67154pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-32.67154ptU^{\prime}\hskip 32.67154ptU}}}{}\,\mathrm{d}s-\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.70146pt\hskip 43.70146pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-43.70146ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.70146ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.70146pt\hskip 43.70146pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-43.70146ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.70146ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-36.26059pt\hskip 36.26059pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-36.26059ptU^{\prime}\hskip 36.26059ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\mathscr{A}\overline{u}(s),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-33.11488pt\hskip 35.64587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-35.64587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.64587ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.64587pt\hskip 35.64587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-35.64587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.64587ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.8744pt\hskip 29.8744pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-29.8744ptU^{\prime}\hskip 29.8744ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.76755pt\hskip 27.76755pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-27.76755ptU^{\prime}\hskip 27.76755ptU}}}{}\,\mathrm{d}s\biggr|

\displaystyle=\biggl|\int_{0}^{t}{\bigl(\nabla{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\nabla P_{n}\psi\bigr)}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}\;\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.0892pt\hskip 41.0892pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-41.0892ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.0892ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.0892pt\hskip 41.0892pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-41.0892ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.0892ptU}}}{{\hskip 32.67154ptU}}}{}\,\mathrm{d}s-\int_{0}^{t}{\bigl(\nabla\overline{u}(s),\nabla\psi\bigr)}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}\;\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.64587pt\hskip 35.64587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-35.64587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.64587ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-35.64587pt\hskip 35.64587pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-35.64587ptU^{\prime}\hskip 35.64587ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle\overline{u}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.8744pt\hskip 27.76755pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-27.76755ptU^{\prime}\hskip 27.76755ptU}}}{}\,\mathrm{d}s\biggr|.

Hence the proof of claim (
5.43
) is complete.
∎

Proof of (ii)
.

Let us temporarily denote

Z=[0,T]\times Y
,
m=\Leb\otimes\nu
and

\displaystyle\xi_{n}(r,y)=F(r,{\overline{u}}_{n}(r,\omega),y),\;\;\;

\displaystyle\xi(r,y)=F(r,\overline{u}(r,\omega),y),\;\;(r,y)\in Z.

Then, by (
4.7
) and Assumption F.
3
we infer that

{\bigl(\xi_{n},\phi\bigr)}\to{\bigl(\xi,\phi\bigr)}\mbox{ in }L^{2}(Z,m),\mbox{for every }\phi\in\mathscr{V}.

(5.49)

Next, by the linear growth condition (
3.5
) in Assumption F.
2
and (
4.12
), there exists a constant
C_{1}=C_{1}(\omega)
such that

\displaystyle\int_{Z}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|\xi_{n}(z)|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-32.68636pt\hskip 32.68636pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-32.68636pt\hskip 32.68636ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|\xi_{n}(z)|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-32.68636pt\hskip 32.68636pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-32.68636pt\hskip 32.68636ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|\xi_{n}(z)|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-23.6506pt\hskip 23.6506pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-23.6506pt\hskip 23.6506ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|\xi_{n}(z)|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-21.86592pt\hskip 21.86592pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-21.86592pt\hskip 21.86592ptH}}}m(\mathrm{d}z)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}\int_{Y}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|\xi_{n}(r,y)|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.53587pt\hskip 41.53587pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.53587pt\hskip 41.53587ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|\xi_{n}(r,y)|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.53587pt\hskip 41.53587pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.53587pt\hskip 41.53587ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|\xi_{n}(r,y)|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.61127pt\hskip 29.61127pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.61127pt\hskip 29.61127ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|\xi_{n}(r,y)|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.26646pt\hskip 27.26646pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-27.26646pt\hskip 27.26646ptH}}}\,\nu(\mathrm{d}y)\,\mathrm{d}r

(5.50)

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{0}^{T}(1+\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|{\overline{u}}_{n}(r)|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-30.78825pt\hskip 30.78825pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-30.78825pt\hskip 30.78825ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|{\overline{u}}_{n}(r)|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-30.78825pt\hskip 30.78825pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-30.78825pt\hskip 30.78825ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|{\overline{u}}_{n}(r)|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-24.83229pt\hskip 24.83229pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-24.83229pt\hskip 24.83229ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|{\overline{u}}_{n}(r)|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-23.43423pt\hskip 23.43423pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-23.43423pt\hskip 23.43423ptH}}})\,\mathrm{d}r\leq CT(1+C_{1}),\;\;n\in\mathbb{N}.

Therefore, we infer that the following stronger version of (
5.49
) holds:

\displaystyle{\bigl(\xi_{n},\phi\bigr)}\to{\bigl(\xi,\phi\bigr)}\mbox{ in }L^{2}(Z,m),\mbox{ for every }\phi\in H.

(5.51)

Next we claim that even stronger than above assertion holds, i.e.

\displaystyle{\bigl(\xi_{n},P_{n}\phi\bigr)}\to{\bigl(\xi,\phi\bigr)}\mbox{ in }L^{2}(Z,m),\mbox{ for every }\phi\in H.

(5.52)

Indeed, let us choose and fix
\phi\in{H}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\|{\bigl(\xi_{n},P_{n}\phi\bigr)}-{\bigl(\xi,\phi\bigr)}\|_{L^{2}(Z,m)}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\bigl(\xi_{n},\phi\bigr)}-{\bigl(\xi,\phi\bigr)}\|_{L^{2}(Z,m)}+\|{\bigl(\xi_{n},P_{n}\phi-\phi\bigr)}\|_{L^{2}(Z,m)}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\bigl(\xi_{n},\phi\bigr)}-{\bigl(\xi,\phi\bigr)}\|_{L^{2}(Z,m)}+\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|P_{n}\phi-\phi|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-42.94662pt\hskip 42.94662pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-42.94662pt\hskip 42.94662ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|P_{n}\phi-\phi|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-42.94662pt\hskip 42.94662pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-42.94662pt\hskip 42.94662ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|P_{n}\phi-\phi|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-28.13283pt\hskip 28.13283pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-28.13283pt\hskip 28.13283ptH}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|P_{n}\phi-\phi|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-25.5101pt\hskip 25.5101pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-25.5101pt\hskip 25.5101ptH}}}\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|\xi_{n}|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.59608pt\hskip 17.59608pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.59608pt\hskip 17.59608ptL^{2}(Z,m)}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|\xi_{n}|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.59608pt\hskip 17.59608pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-17.59608pt\hskip 17.59608ptL^{2}(Z,m)}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|\xi_{n}|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-13.2929pt\hskip 13.2929pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-13.2929pt\hskip 13.2929ptL^{2}(Z,m)}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|\xi_{n}|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-12.82072pt\hskip 12.82072pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-12.82072pt\hskip 12.82072ptL^{2}(Z,m)}}}

The right-hand side converges to
0
because of (
5.51
) and (
5.50
).
This completes the proof of assertion (
5.44
).
Observe that (
5.45
) follows from (
5.52
) because it implies that

\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|{\bigl(\xi_{n},P_{n}\phi\bigr)}|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-48.16783pt\hskip 48.16783pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-48.16783pt\hskip 48.16783ptL^{2}(Z,m)}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|{\bigl(\xi_{n},P_{n}\phi\bigr)}|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-48.16783pt\hskip 48.16783pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-48.16783pt\hskip 48.16783ptL^{2}(Z,m)}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|{\bigl(\xi_{n},P_{n}\phi\bigr)}|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.57562pt\hskip 40.57562pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-40.57562pt\hskip 40.57562ptL^{2}(Z,m)}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|{\bigl(\xi_{n},P_{n}\phi\bigr)}|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-38.33064pt\hskip 38.33064pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-38.33064pt\hskip 38.33064ptL^{2}(Z,m)}}}\to\mathchoice{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{|{\bigl(\xi,\phi\bigr)}|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.47214pt\hskip 29.47214pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.47214pt\hskip 29.47214ptL^{2}(Z,m)}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\textstyle{|{\bigl(\xi,\phi\bigr)}|}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.47214pt\hskip 29.47214pt2}_{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-29.47214pt\hskip 29.47214ptL^{2}(Z,m)}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{|{\bigl(\xi,\phi\bigr)}|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-24.59993pt\hskip 24.59993pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-24.59993pt\hskip 24.59993ptL^{2}(Z,m)}}}{{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{|{\bigl(\xi,\phi\bigr)}|}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-23.27782pt\hskip 23.27782pt2}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-23.27782pt\hskip 23.27782ptL^{2}(Z,m)}}}

which is equivalent to (
5.45
).
This completes our alternative proof of claim (
5.44
).
∎

Proof of (iii)
.

We will prove first the following, easier than (
5.46
), assertion.

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle B({\overline{u}}_{n}(s)),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-57.50938pt\hskip 57.50938pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-57.50938ptU^{\prime}\hskip 57.50938ptU}}}{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\bigl\langle B({\overline{u}}_{n}(s)),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-57.50938pt\hskip 57.50938pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-57.50938ptU^{\prime}\hskip 57.50938ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle B({\overline{u}}_{n}(s)),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-47.89479pt\hskip B({\overline{u}}_{n}(s)),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-43.95119pt\hskip 43.95119pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-43.95119ptU^{\prime}\hskip 43.95119ptU}}}\;\mathchoice{{\hskip 8.90308pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.0892pt\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-41.0892pt\hskip 41.0892pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-41.0892ptU^{\prime}\hskip 41.0892ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-34.7784pt\hskip 34.7784pt}_{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip-7.96948pt\hskip-34.7784ptU^{\prime}\hskip 34.7784ptU}}}{{\hskip 7.96948pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bigl\langle{\overline{u}}_{n}(s),\phi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptscriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-32.67154pt\hskip B(\overline{u}(s)),\psi\bigr\rangle}$}^{\scriptstyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-52.06606pt\hskip 52.06606pt}_{\scriptstyle\hskip-8.90308pt\hskip-52.06606ptU^{\prime}\hskip 52.06606ptU}}}{{\hskip (5.53)

We begin with a case when
\psi,\phi\in\mathscr{V}
. Then we choose
R\in\mathbb{N}
such that
\supp(\phi)\subset B_{R}
.
We have the following chain of inequalities, see the proof of
[

, Lemma B.1]
,
